    ---
    name: ponytail
    description: >
      Lazy senior dev mode: the smallest change that fully solves the task, and a
      reply a busy human understands in one read. Use on any coding task (writing,
      fixing, refactoring, reviewing, choosing dependencies) and when the user says
      "ponytail", "be lazy", "simplest solution", "yagni", or complains about
      over-engineering or bloat. Levels: lite, full (default), ultra.
    argument-hint: "[lite|full|ultra|off]"
    license: MIT
    ---
    
    # Ponytail
    
    You are a lazy senior developer. The best code is the code never written. You solve the whole problem with the least new code. End your reply with one or two lines: what you skipped or did not check, and any risk the user must know.
    
    Active for the whole session until the user says "stop ponytail" or "normal mode". Switch level: `/ponytail lite|full|ultra`.
    
    ## Before you write
    
    Read the task and the code it touches. List every place your change must reach: callers, tests, fixtures, config, exports. Check what your change could break for users: data it would destroy or expose, callers that stop working. That is scope. Extra features are not.
    
    ## The smallest complete change
    
    Take the first option that fully works:
    
    1. Does it need to exist? Skip features, options and flexibility nobody asked for, and name them in one line. A vague request ("build me X") gets the smallest version that does the core job.
    2. Already in this codebase (a helper, component, service, pattern)? Use it the way the surrounding code does.
    3. Standard library or a platform feature? Use it, unless the project has its own. A house component beats a native widget.
    4. An installed dependency? Use it. Never add a dependency for a few lines.
    5. Can it be one line a reader gets at a glance? One line.
    6. Otherwise: the minimum code that works.
    
    - Be lazy about the solution, never about the change itself: finish every part the task needs, including the callers, tests and fixtures your change breaks.
    - No abstraction, wrapper, type conversion, option, config, boilerplate or "for later" code nobody asked for. Keep values in the form the platform already gives you. Deletion beats addition. Keep the structure the codebase already has: its layers, interfaces and conventions.
    - Minimize the complete diff across all affected source files, preserving existing code, style, names and structure wherever they already work. Do not rewrite, reformat, modernize or switch patterns for a supposedly better approach unless explicitly requested or necessary to meet the task, correctness or safety. Do not count generated files when assessing diff size. A one-liner that needs decoding is not short.
    - Comment only the why the code cannot show, in one line.
    - Bug fix: before you edit, grep every caller of the function you touch, then fix the root cause once in the shared code.
    - Code you move or merge keeps its error handling and validation.
    - Between options of equal size, take the one that is correct on edge cases.
    - Lazy code without its check is unfinished: new non-trivial logic (a branch, a loop, a parser, money or security, or a whole new script or app) leaves one small test or an assert-based self-check. Trivial changes need none.
    - A shortcut with a known limit gets a code comment in this form: `shortcut: <the limit>, <when to upgrade>`.
    
    Never cut: validation at trust boundaries, error handling that prevents data loss, security, accessibility, the calibration real hardware needs, anything the user asked for.
    
    ## Levels
    
    | Level | Behavior |
    |-------|----------|
    | **lite** | Build what was asked. Name the smaller option in one line and let the user pick. |
    | **full** | The rules above. Default. |
    | **ultra** | Also question the request: before building, push back on any part the need does not justify. |
    
    
    Default: "full"
    
    ---
    name: kl-solid-coding
    description: "Apply SOLID and KL's selected Go proverbs on every coding task in any language: write, fix, refactor, review, choose dependencies. Always use alongside Ponytail."
    metadata:
      display-name: KL-SOLID-CODING
    ---
    
    # SOLID coding
    
    # ALWAYS
    
    - Every coding task: load [Ponytail](../ponytail/SKILL.md) + relevant language/domain skills.
    - Current need; Ponytail's smallest complete solution. Go concurrency/panic guidance requires equivalent language semantics.
    
    ## SOLID meanings
    
    - **S — Single Responsibility Principle:** one reason to change per module, tied to one actor/business concern; group code changing together, separate independent concerns.
    - **O — Open/Closed Principle:** open for extension, closed for modification; extend stable boundaries without changing established behavior. Extension points require demonstrated variation.
    - **L — Liskov Substitution Principle:** substitutes preserve contracts: accepted inputs, outputs, invariants, failure behavior; no stronger preconditions/weaker postconditions.
    - **I — Interface Segregation Principle:** clients depend only on needed operations; small, cohesive, consumer-focused contracts.
    - **D — Dependency Inversion Principle:** high-level policy + low-level details depend on abstractions; details implement policy-facing contracts. Supply concrete dependencies at composition boundaries.
    
    ## Go proverbs
    
    - Don't communicate by sharing memory, share memory by communicating.
    - Concurrency is not parallelism.
    - Channels orchestrate; mutexes serialize.
    - The bigger the interface, the weaker the abstraction.
    - A little copying is better than a little dependency.
    - Clear is better than clever.
    - Reflection is never clear.
    - Don't just check errors, handle them gracefully.
    - Design the architecture, name the components, document the details.
    - Don't panic.
    
    # NEVER
    
    - No speculative interfaces, inheritance, layers, or dependencies for SOLID compliance; preserve required contracts, validation, error handling.
    
    # Taste
    
    - Use names that state the domain purpose of methods, variables and collections; avoid unexplained names such as `rules` or `reglas` outside framework-required contracts. Preserve framework names and existing public contracts.
    - Prefer consistent domain-first prefixes that group related variables/settings like namespaces, following the language casing convention. Example: `panel_zoom_enabled`, `panel_zoom_gesture_type`, `panel_zoom_factor`, `panel_zoom_slider_min`; keep the distinct panel-opening setting `panel_gesture` separate. Avoid reversed or ambiguous names such as `panel_gesture_zoom`. When renaming persisted keys, migrate existing values and update all callers.
    - Keep helpers private/file-local and at the end of their class or file. Shared behavior belongs in a named domain collaborator, not cross-file `helper_*` calls or generic utility bags.
    - Avoid unexplained numeric IDs and abstract values in conditions. Prefer meaningful strings where the domain permits; otherwise use a named constant or a preceding comment explaining the case. Preserve required external IDs.
    - PHP: import classes at the top of the file instead of repeating fully qualified class names inside methods, unless qualification is needed to disambiguate.
    - Add PHPDoc for non-obvious contracts, data shapes, constraints and business reasons; do not restate the signature or narrate the implementation. Check history when assessing stale comments; retain explanations of current constraints.
    - Keep related validation and special cases understandable together: do not silently skip several fields and handle them elsewhere under an unexplained check for only one of them.
    - Prefer a little local duplication over a dependency on a trivial wrapper that adds no meaningful responsibility. Do not extract a module solely to wrap a few lines.
    - Keep behavior specific to one case in its owning module or screen. Extract it only for demonstrated reuse or a boundary that materially improves clarity or correctness; one usage alone does not justify another file or layer.
    - Place reusable components in the project's component area, grouped in subfolders by module, domain or category. Keep screens in the screen area and compose them from those components. Apply these rules independently of language or framework.
    ✓ • 18ms

    ---
    name: kl-backend
    description: "Apply KL's backend architecture and testing preferences when building, reviewing, or refactoring services, APIs, workers, and bots in any language or framework: explicit constructor injection, thin entry points, real service tests, and replaceable IO."
    metadata:
      display-name: KL-backend
      version: "1.0.0"
    ---
    
    # Backend
    
    Backend architecture, DI, persistence, tests; project-native mechanisms.
    
    Conditions/exceptions apply; preferences remain preferences.
    
    # ALWAYS
    
    - Explicit constructor/initializer dependencies; inject DB, filesystem, network, collaborators. Native DI when available; resolve at composition roots/test setup. Centralize identifiers; separate production/test wiring; class types where supported, tokens for interfaces/config/ambiguity.
    - Container builds service graph, including tests; override changed IO/config. Small contracts for replaceable IO; concrete services when interfaces add no substitution value.
    - Deliberate lifetimes: infrastructure/intentional queues may be singletons; isolate per-execution context. Typed data-service facade optional when simplifying callers.
    - Thin controllers/commands/handlers/jobs; cohesive services; testable stateless calculation/formatting helpers. Existing lifecycle base class, otherwise native middleware/guards/interceptors; inheritance only for justified shared lifecycle.
    - Map differing DB/domain names, nullability, booleans, encodings at persistence boundary. Inject connection into data services; direct ORM services acceptable.
    - Preserve expected absence/rejection versus unexpected failure and existing result contracts. Validate untrusted entry input; useful error boundary, diagnostics, caller feedback. Promised atomic multi-write transition → datastore transaction.
    - Interactive entrypoints: meaningful progress/success/failure feedback. Localization optional unless requested/existing.
    - Colocated tests, project runner; observable outcomes + important failure paths. Persistence: prefer real services/queries, isolated DB, actual schema/migrations; ask SQLite versus project DB when choosing. In-memory DB only with compatible semantics; actual engine when constraints/transactions/dialect matter.
    - Explicit fixtures via reusable domain builders/seed helpers; reset mutable data per test, retain/reseed lookups. Shared resettable DB → sequential tests or separate parallel DBs.
    - Reuse test composition; override DB/external IO. Resolve subject through child container/native testing module; bind resolved instance only when assertions require identical instance. Testing modules: import relevant module, override external providers before compilation, then resolve.
    - Fake filesystem/messaging/remote APIs at boundaries; real subject/business collaborators. Pure dependency-free helpers may be instantiated directly.
    - Drive input boundary; assert responses/events/messages + persisted state. Relevant invalid input, missing data, rejection/cancellation, permissions covered.
    - Public lifecycle cleanup: queues, timers, spies/clocks, resources; singleton state needs separate reset. Native provider registration/tokens/overrides/lifecycle; native request/auth extension points.
    
    # NEVER
    
    - No business-logic global dependency lookup, injected service-locator container, second DI container, or parallel manually reconstructed test graph.
    - No request data in singleton fields; child container ≠ state isolation. Grouped facade ≠ transaction or mandatory layer.
    - No unnecessary IO interfaces, generic repositories, duplicate CRUD layers, or unjustified inheritance; no mandated language/ORM/DI/runner/framework.
    - No leaked persistence conversions/sensitive public error internals; infrastructure failure ≠ success.
    - No private-method/exact-call-sequence tests or private-collection cleanup. No handwritten RAM repositories replacing realistic persistence tests. DB row reset ≠ singleton reset.
    
    # Taste
    
    - Tabular/report data: preserve named fields through queries, preparation, templates and exports. Define columns by field name, with display/export order explicit; avoid discarding keys and matching values to headers/styles by numeric position. Preserve external positional formats at their serialization boundary.

The default layout has saved SQL files above connections on the far left, SQL in the center, the database explorer on the right, results beneath SQL, and a full-width activity bar at the bottom. **Shift+F3** toggles the live diagram: connections disappear and SQL/diagram use 30% / 70% of the width by default, with results still beneath SQL. A compact terminal uses panel toggles above the activity bar.
    docs/query-mode.md:11:3. Press **gC** in Normal mode or run `:FoxQueryConnections` to focus the connection sidebar. **a** adds, **e** edits, **d** deletes a profile, **Enter** opens all connection details; **Enter** again connects. **D** disconnects. SQLite opens the existing file explorer at your project root: Enter opens folders or selects a database file; Esc cancels. MySQL/MariaDB and PostgreSQL need host, port, database, and user. Choose **MySQL / MariaDB** in the engine picker for MariaDB (`engine: "mysql"`); no separate driver installation is needed. Enter the password once in the centered, masked dialog. Use **Ctrl+V** to paste, **Enter** to confirm, or **Esc** to cancel. Passwords are saved locally under `.foxnvim/query/private/passwords/`. To use an environment variable, configure `password_env` in `connections.json`. Editing a connection lets you replace its saved password. Leave the field blank to keep it.
    docs/query-mode.md:14:6. Queries run manually with **Ctrl+Enter** or **F5** in Normal/Insert mode. **Ctrl+Shift+Enter** runs the same statement in a centered results popup using 90% of the screen width and height; **q** or **Esc** closes it. Typing SQL or moving the cursor does not execute it. **Ctrl+Space** or **Ctrl+X Ctrl+O** opens completion in Insert mode. **F3** restores the editor without closing SQL sessions.
    docs/query-mode.md:24:In SQL, **Shift+K** (`K`) on a column such as `ca.nombre` shows its source table and cached type in a compact popup. Aliases, bare columns, UTF-8 names such as `año`, and correlated references resolve in the nearest query scope. Ambiguous bare names ask for an alias. On a table name or table alias, Shift+K opens the same table inspector as Enter in the explorer. Functions retain their dialect documentation. Schema lookup uses cached metadata and does not execute SQL; computed output aliases and unresolved names fall back to normal hover handling. The palette action **Query: Inspect SQL Symbol at Cursor** and `:FoxQueryHover` provide the same action.
    docs/query-mode.md:28:Automatic execution is disabled by default. If explicitly enabled with `:FoxQueryAutoRun`, it considers only the statement at the cursor. It never submits UPDATE/DELETE/INSERT, WITH, or EXPLAIN automatically. The worker independently validates SQL and enforces read-only access; SELECT INTO is rejected. Unchanged SQL is not repeatedly submitted, requests are serialized, hidden projects do not auto-run, and bound parameters require a manual run. `:FoxQueryAutoRun` toggles automatic execution for the project in RAM. SELECT can still do expensive work or invoke server functions; use restricted credentials and disable auto-run when appropriate.
    docs/query-mode.md:34:**Ctrl+Enter** or **F5** runs the statement at the cursor and opens results along the bottom of that SQL pane. Accent lines mark the current SQL statement. **Ctrl+J** or **gR** focuses results from SQL, **Shift+H/J/K/L** moves between cells, and **Ctrl+K** returns to SQL. **q** hides results. Pane navigation from elsewhere lands in the SQL editor. **Ctrl+Shift+Enter** also opens the large results popup. Switching environments restores an open Query view or SQL split and its open Query popups when you return.
    docs/query-mode.md:80:| `:FoxQueryRun statement` | Execute the statement under the SQL cursor. |
    docs/query-mode.md:81:| `:FoxQueryRunPopup [statement/selection/section/file]` | Execute SQL and open its results in a 90% popup. |
    docs/query-mode.md:87:| `:FoxQueryRun selection` | Execute the last character/line selection; block selections are rejected. |
    docs/query-mode.md:88:| `:FoxQueryRun section`, `:FoxQueryRun file` | Execute an explicit section or file, in order, stopping on error. |
    docs/query-mode.md:118:SELECT o.id, o.total FROM orders o WHERE o.customer_id = ?;
    docs/query-mode.md:137:Use Enter on a table box to inspect it; the cursor position determines the left/right box. Inspector actions use the relationship card under the cursor (or the inspected table in its property section). Enter opens another table inspector on top; **q** or **Esc** returns to the previous inspector with its cursor and scroll position preserved. Moving focus outside the inspector stack dismisses it. `J` offers Insert JOIN, `n` opens notes, `b` browses rows, **B** browses in the results popup, and `g` opens the neighborhood. Query choices use compact floating lists: j/k or arrows move, Enter selects, Esc cancels, and native `/` searches. They do not use Telescope. A single FK mapping skips the mapping picker; after the alias prompt, one SQL preview list offers INNER and LEFT JOINs and inserts the chosen SQL. JOIN choices display the actual column mapping, such as `PlayerKisses.member_1 = Player.id`, rather than internal FK IDs. The alias defaults to table initials (`p`, then `p2` if occupied). MySQL and SQLite JOINs leave ordinary identifiers unquoted; keywords and names with special characters remain quoted so the generated SQL stays valid. PostgreSQL retains its existing quoting. Joining always requires an explicit reviewed edit; clicking a node does not rewrite or execute SQL.
    docs/query-mode.md:143:SQL FILES uses Neo-tree's tree renderer, directory expansion, and font icons in a dedicated Query source. File creation, renaming, and deletion still use Query's validation and modified-buffer checks. The source includes unsaved SQL consoles. The editor sidebar remains separate, and environment switches restore the last editor window before reopening Query panels. Delayed Neo-tree navigation and renders stay in their owning tab.
    docs/query-mode.md:163:Read-only behavior is enforced with SQLite read-only URI/query-only mode, MySQL session transaction read-only mode, and PostgreSQL read-only transactions. Use restricted database credentials too: SELECT functions and external server effects are not comprehensively controlled by statement classification. SQL writes, editable grids, transaction UI, routine execution, native placeholder formats, automatic recovery drafts, full SQL history, and virtual relationships remain outside this first implementation. Session settings/temporary objects cannot be created through the read-only SQL guard.
    docs/query-mode.md:169:Automated tests use disposable SQLite fixtures for binding, batches, metadata, read-only enforcement, row limits, cancellation, and actual Neovim-to-Go transport. Lua tests cover scopes, aliases, composite JOINs, diagram roles, project storage, view switching, and session exclusion.
    docs/query-mode.md:171:Database execution uses the bundled Go worker. Backend tests use real disposable SQLite files and cover binding, Unicode, numeric precision, binary cells, SQL guards, metadata, engine-enforced read-only access, attachment rejection, missing-file handling, row/byte caps, cancellation, protocol recovery, and full error diagnostics. Lua tests exercise the bundled executable using a committed SQLite fixture.

    $ cat tools/repos/fox-query/sql.go; sed -n '100,330p' tools/repos/fox-query/driver.go; sed -n '2140,2305p' lua/fox/query/init.lua; cat tools/repos/fox-query/README.md; git status --short
    package main
    
    import (
        "fmt"
        "regexp"
        "strings"
        "unicode"
    )
    
    type token struct {
        kind, text string
        start, end int
    }
    type statement struct {
        sql       string
        positions []int
    }
    
    var word = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9$]*|^[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?`)
    var dollar = regexp.MustCompile(`^\$(?:[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*)?\$`)
    
    func lex(s, engine string) ([]token, error) {
        out := []token{}
        for i := 0; i < len(s); {
            start := i
            c := s[i : i+1]
            if unicode.IsSpace(rune(s[i])) {
                i++
                continue
            }
            if strings.HasPrefix(s[i:], "--") || strings.HasPrefix(engine, "mysql") && c == "#" {
                for i < len(s) && s[i] != '\n' {
                    i++
                }
                continue
            }
            if strings.HasPrefix(s[i:], "/*") {
                if strings.HasPrefix(s[i:], "/*!") || strings.HasPrefix(s[i:], "/*M!") {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Executable SQL comments are not supported")
                }
                depth := 1
                i += 2
                for i < len(s) && depth > 0 {
                    if strings.HasPrefix(s[i:], "/*") {
                        if engine != "postgres" {
                            return nil, fmt.Errorf("Nested SQL comments are unsupported in this dialect")
                        }
                        depth++
                        i += 2
                    } else if strings.HasPrefix(s[i:], "*/") {
                        depth--
                        i += 2
                    } else {
                        i++
                    }
                }
                if depth > 0 {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unfinished SQL comment")
                }
                continue
            }
            if engine == "postgres" {
                if tag := dollar.FindString(s[i:]); tag != "" {
                    end := strings.Index(s[i+len(tag):], tag)
                    if end < 0 {
                        return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unfinished dollar-quoted SQL")
                    }
                    i += len(tag)*2 + end
                    out = append(out, token{"string", s[start:i], start, i})
                    continue
                }
            }
            if strings.Contains("'\"`[", c) {
                close := c
                if c == "[" {
                    close = "]"
                }
                i++
                done := false
                for i < len(s) {
                    if s[i:i+1] == close {
                        i++
                        if i < len(s) && s[i:i+1] == close {
                            i++
                            continue
                        }
                        done = true
                        break
                    }
                    if s[i] == '\\' && c == "'" && (engine == "mysql" || engine == "postgres" && start > 0 && strings.EqualFold(s[start-1:start], "e")) {
                        i += 2
                    } else {
                        i++
                    }
                }
                if !done {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unfinished SQL quote")
                }
                kind := "identifier"
                if c == "'" {
                    kind = "string"
                }
                out = append(out, token{kind, s[start:i], start, i})
                continue
            }
            if w := word.FindString(s[i:]); w != "" {
                i += len(w)
                out = append(out, token{"word", w, start, i})
            } else {
                i++
                out = append(out, token{"symbol", c, start, i})
            }
        }
        return out, nil
    }
    
    func prepare(s, engine string) ([]statement, error) {
        if len(s) > 1024*1024 {
            return nil, fmt.Errorf("SQL must be text of at most 1 MiB")
        }
        tokens, err := lex(s, engine)
        if err != nil {
            return nil, err
        }
        forbidden := " INSERT UPDATE DELETE MERGE CREATE ALTER DROP TRUNCATE ATTACH DETACH PRAGMA COPY CALL DO SET RESET INTO LOCK UNLOCK GRANT REVOKE VACUUM ANALYZE ANALYSE LOAD "
        pieces := []string{}
        first, depth := 0, 0
        for _, t := range tokens {
            if t.kind == "word" && strings.Contains(forbidden, " "+strings.ToUpper(t.text)+" ") {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("Read-only querying only; unsupported operation: %s", strings.ToUpper(t.text))
            }
            if t.kind != "symbol" {
                continue
            }
            switch t.text {
            case "(":
                depth++
            case ")":
                depth--
                if depth < 0 {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unbalanced SQL parentheses")
                }
            case ";":
                if depth != 0 {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unsupported SQL statement body")
                }
                pieces = append(pieces, s[first:t.start])
                first = t.end
            }
        }
        if depth != 0 {
            return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unbalanced SQL parentheses")
        }
        pieces = append(pieces, s[first:])
        out := []statement{}
        for _, piece := range pieces {
            ts, _ := lex(piece, engine)
            if len(ts) == 0 {
                continue
            }
            if ts[0].kind != "word" || !strings.Contains(" SELECT WITH EXPLAIN ", " "+strings.ToUpper(ts[0].text)+" ") {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("Only SELECT, read-only WITH, and EXPLAIN are supported")
            }
            st := statement{sql: piece}
            for i, t := range ts {
                if t.kind != "symbol" || t.text != "?" {
                    continue
                }
                if engine == "postgres" {
                    if i+1 < len(ts) && (ts[i+1].text == "|" || ts[i+1].text == "&") {
                        continue
                    }
                    if i > 0 {
                        p := ts[i-1]
                        if p.kind == "identifier" || p.kind == "string" || p.text == ")" || p.text == "]" || p.kind == "word" && !strings.Contains(" SELECT WHERE AND OR WHEN THEN ELSE LIMIT OFFSET BY AS DISTINCT BETWEEN LIKE ILIKE IS IN ", " "+strings.ToUpper(p.text)+" ") {
                            continue
                        }
                    }
                }
                if i+1 < len(ts) && ts[i+1].start == t.end && ts[i+1].text[0] >= '0' && ts[i+1].text[0] <= '9' {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Use plain ? placeholders, not numbered placeholders")
                }
                st.positions = append(st.positions, t.start)
            }
            out = append(out, st)
        }
        if len(out) == 0 {
            return nil, fmt.Errorf("No SQL statements to execute")
        }
        if len(out) > 100 {
            return nil, fmt.Errorf("At most 100 statements per run")
        }
        return out, nil
    }
    func boundSQL(s statement, engine string) string {
        if engine != "postgres" {
            return s.sql
        }
        var b strings.Builder
        first := 0
        for i, pos := range s.positions {
            b.WriteString(s.sql[first:pos])
            fmt.Fprintf(&b, "$%d", i+1)
            first = pos + 1
        }
        b.WriteString(s.sql[first:])
        return b.String()
    }
            cfg.WriteTimeout = 10 * time.Second
            cfg.Params = map[string]string{"charset": "utf8mb4"}

            }
            u.Host = net.JoinHostPort(host, strconv.Itoa(port))
            q := url.Values{"connect_timeout": {"10"}}
            if p.SSLMode != "" {
                q.Set("sslmode", p.SSLMode)
            }
            if p.SSLRootCert != "" {
                q.Set("sslrootcert", p.SSLRootCert)
            }
            u.RawQuery = q.Encode()
            cfg, e := pgx.ParseConfig(u.String())
            if e != nil {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("Invalid PostgreSQL connection settings")
            }
            db = stdlib.OpenDB(*cfg)
        default:
            return nil, fmt.Errorf("Choose sqlite, mysql, or postgres")
        }
        if err != nil {
            return nil, err
        }
        d.db = db
        db.SetMaxOpenConns(1)
        d.conn, err = db.Conn(ctx)
        if err != nil {
            d.close()
            return nil, fmt.Errorf("Database connection failed; check host, credentials, TLS, and permissions")
        }
        success := false
        defer func() {
            if !success {
                d.close()
            }
        }()
        version := ""
        switch p.Engine {
        case "sqlite":
            _, err = sqlite.Limit(d.conn, sqlite3.SQLITE_LIMIT_ATTACHED, 0)
            if err == nil {
                err = d.conn.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT sqlite_version()").Scan(&version)
            }
        case "mysql":
            _, err = d.conn.ExecContext(ctx, "SET SESSION TRANSACTION READ ONLY")
            if err == nil {
                var mode string
                err = d.conn.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SELECT VERSION(), @@SESSION.sql_mode").Scan(&version, &mode)
                if strings.Contains(","+mode+",", ",NO_BACKSLASH_ESCAPES,") {
                    d.syntax = "mysql-no-backslash"
                }
            }
        case "postgres":
            _, err = d.conn.ExecContext(ctx, "SET default_transaction_read_only = on")
            if err == nil {
                schemas := p.Schemas
                if len(schemas) == 0 {
                    schema := p.Schema
                    if schema == "" {
                        schema = "public"
                    }
                    schemas = []string{schema}
                }
                quoted := []string{}
                for _, s := range schemas {
                    quoted = append(quoted, quote(s))
                }
                _, err = d.conn.ExecContext(ctx, "SET search_path TO "+strings.Join(quoted, ","))
            }
            if err == nil {
                err = d.conn.QueryRowContext(ctx, "SHOW server_version").Scan(&version)
            }
        }
        if err != nil {
            return nil, fmt.Errorf("Could not enforce a read-only session; verify server compatibility and restricted credentials")
        }
        success = true
        return map[string]any{"engine": p.Engine, "syntax": d.syntax, "version": version, "read_only": "engine-enforced read-only; use restricted credentials"}, nil
    }
    func quote(s string) string { return `"` + strings.ReplaceAll(s, `"`, `""`) + `"` }
    func valueJSON(v any, typ string) (any, bool) {
        if v == nil {
            return nil, false
        }
        switch x := v.(type) {
        case []byte:
            binary := typ == "" || strings.Contains(typ, "BLOB") || strings.Contains(typ, "BINARY") || typ == "BYTEA"
            if binary {
                n := len(x)
                if len(x) > 4096 {
                    x = x[:4096]
                }
                return map[string]any{"type": "binary", "base64": base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(x), "bytes": n}, false
            }
            return string(x), isText(typ)
        case string:
            return x, isText(typ)
        case bool:
            return x, false
        case time.Time:
            return x.Format(time.RFC3339Nano), false
        default:
            return fmt.Sprint(x), false
        }
    }
    func isText(t string) bool {
        return t == "" || strings.Contains(t, "CHAR") || strings.Contains(t, "TEXT") || t == "NAME" || t == "UNKNOWN" || t == "VARCHAR"
    }
    
    type resultSet struct {
        Columns   []string `json:"columns"`
        Rows      [][]any  `json:"rows"`
        Strings   [][]bool `json:"strings"`
        Truncated bool     `json:"truncated"`
    }
    
    func (d *driver) execute(ctx context.Context, s string, params []any, emit func(any), id any) (any, error) {
        ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
        defer cancel()
        statements, err := prepare(s, d.syntax)
        if err != nil {
            return nil, err
        }
        count := 0
        for _, st := range statements {
            count += len(st.positions)
        }
        if len(params) != count {
            return nil, fmt.Errorf("Parameter count does not match SQL")
        }
        for i, p := range params {
            if n, ok := p.(json.Number); ok {
                if v, e := n.Int64(); e == nil {
                    params[i] = v
                } else if v, e := n.Float64(); e == nil {
                    params[i] = v
                } else {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Invalid numeric parameter")
                }
            }
        }
        sets := []resultSet{}
        offset, totalRows, totalBytes := 0, 0, 0
        for _, st := range statements {
            rows, e := d.conn.QueryContext(ctx, boundSQL(st, d.profile.Engine), params[offset:offset+len(st.positions)]...)
            offset += len(st.positions)
            if e != nil {
                return nil, e
            }
            set := resultSet{Rows: [][]any{}, Strings: [][]bool{}}
            set.Columns, e = rows.Columns()
            if e != nil {
                rows.Close()
                return nil, e
            }
            types, e := rows.ColumnTypes()
            if e != nil {
                rows.Close()
                return nil, e
            }
            for rows.Next() {
                raw := make([]any, len(set.Columns))
                dest := make([]any, len(raw))
                for i := range raw {
                    dest[i] = &raw[i]
                }
                if e = rows.Scan(dest...); e != nil {
                    break
                }
                encoded := make([]any, len(raw))
                flags := make([]bool, len(raw))
                for i, v := range raw {
                    encoded[i], flags[i] = valueJSON(v, strings.ToUpper(types[i].DatabaseTypeName()))
                    if _, ok := v.(string); ok && d.profile.Engine == "sqlite" {
                        flags[i] = true
                    }
                }
                b, _ := json.Marshal(encoded)
                if totalRows >= 1000 || totalBytes+len(b) > 8*1024*1024 {
                    set.Truncated = true
                    break
                }
                set.Rows = append(set.Rows, encoded)
                set.Strings = append(set.Strings, flags)
                totalRows++
                totalBytes += len(b)
                if totalRows%100 == 0 {
                    emit(map[string]any{"id": id, "event": "fetching", "rows": totalRows})
                }
            }
            if e == nil {
                e = rows.Err()
            }
            // Closing MySQL streaming rows drains them; discard the connection on a cap instead.
            if set.Truncated && d.profile.Engine == "mysql" {
                cancel()
            }
            rows.Close()
            if set.Truncated && d.profile.Engine == "mysql" {
                d.close()
            }
            if e != nil {
                return nil, e
            }
            sets = append(sets, set)
            if set.Truncated {
                break
                notify("Block selections are unsupported", vim.log.levels.WARN)
                return
            end
            local first, last = vim.api.nvim_buf_get_mark(buf, "<"), vim.api.nvim_buf_get_mark(buf, ">")
            if first[1] == 0 or last[1] == 0 then
                notify("Select SQL first", vim.log.levels.WARN)
                return
            end
            local lines = vim.api.nvim_buf_get_lines(buf, first[1] - 1, last[1], false)
            if vim.fn.visualmode() ~= "V" then
                lines[#lines] = lines[#lines]:sub(1, last[2] + 1)
                lines[1] = lines[1]:sub(first[2] + 1)
            end
            selection = table.concat(lines, "\n")
        end
        local query, scope_err
        if supplied_sql then
            query = supplied_sql
        else
            local engine = require("fox.langs.sql.dialect").read(buf)
                or vim.b[buf].fox_sql_dialect
                or item.syntax
                or profile.engine
            query, scope_err = sql.scope(text, offset, kind or "statement", selection, engine)
        end
        if not query then
            notify(scope_err, vim.log.levels.WARN)
            return
        end
        if not connected then
            item.pending_run = function()
                if
                    owns_state(state)
                    and (not state.inline or inline.owns(state.sql_win, buf))
                    and storage.root() == state.root
                    and console(state) == item
                    and valid(buf, "buf")
                    and item.client
                    and item.client.connected
                    and storage.fingerprint(item.profile) == storage.fingerprint(profile)
                then
                    M.run(kind, query, supplied_params, source_table, automatic, popup, state)
                end
            end
            if not (item.client and item.client.connecting) then
                M.connect(profile, state)
                if not (item.client and item.client.connecting) then
                    item.pending_run = nil
                end
            end
            return
        end
        local connection = item.client
        connection.busy, item.status = true, "validating " .. (kind or "statement")
        connection:request("prepare", { sql = query }, function(prepared, prepare_err, prepare_detail)
            if prepare_err then
                connection.busy = false
                item.status = prepare_err
                item.error_detail = prepare_detail and prepare_detail ~= vim.NIL and prepare_detail or prepare_err
                notify(item.error_detail, vim.log.levels.ERROR)
                M.refresh_view(state)
                if item.auto_pending then
                    item.auto_pending = nil
                    M.autorun(state)
                end
                return
            end
            if automatic and prepared.parameters > 0 then
                connection.busy, item.status = false, "Parameters needed: Ctrl+Enter / F5 runs with values"
                M.refresh_view(state)
                return
            end
            item.auto_sql, item.auto_connection = query, connection
            local params = supplied_params or {}
            local function execute()
                if item.client ~= connection or not valid(buf, "buf") then
                    connection.busy = false
                    return
                end
                item.params, item.executed_sql = params, query
                item.error_detail = nil
                item.started, item.status = (vim.uv or vim.loop).hrtime(), "running " .. prepared.statements .. " statement(s)"
                item.execution = (item.execution or 0) + 1
                local execution = item.execution
                local result_label = "run #"
                    .. execution
                    .. " | "
                    .. item.profile.id
                    .. " | "
                    .. os.date("%H:%M:%S")
                    .. " | "
                    .. vim.fn.fnamemodify(vim.api.nvim_buf_get_name(buf), ":t")
                connection:request("execute", { sql = query, params = params }, function(result, err, detail)
                    connection.busy = false
                    item.error_detail = err and (detail and detail ~= vim.NIL and detail or err) or nil
                    if err then
                        notify(item.error_detail, vim.log.levels.ERROR)
                    end
                    local seconds = ((vim.uv or vim.loop).hrtime() - item.started) / 1e9
                    item.status = (err or "completed") .. string.format(" | %.2fs", seconds)
                    if result then
                        item.result = result
                        item.result_lines, item.result_label = nil, result_label
                        item.result_table = source_table
                        if result.disconnected then
                            connection.connected = false
                        end
                    end
                    M.refresh_view(state)
                    if result and popup then
                        M.results_popup(state, item)
                    end
                    if item.auto_pending then
                        item.auto_pending = nil
                        M.autorun(state)
                    end
                end)
                local function elapsed()
                    if item.execution ~= execution or not connection.busy or item.client ~= connection then
                        return
                    end
                    item.status = string.format("running / fetching | %.1fs", ((vim.uv or vim.loop).hrtime() - item.started) / 1e9)
                    if valid(state.activity_buf, "buf") then
                        M.draw_activity(state)
                    end
                    vim.defer_fn(elapsed, 500)
                end
                vim.defer_fn(elapsed, 500)
                M.refresh_view(state)
            end
            if supplied_params or prepared.parameters == 0 then
                execute()
                return
            end
            local engine = require("fox.langs.sql.dialect").read(buf) or item.syntax or profile.engine
            local bound, binding_err = parameters.bound(state.root, vim.b[buf].fox_query_document, query, engine)
            if not bound or #bound ~= prepared.parameters then
                connection.busy = false
                item.status = binding_err or "Placeholder detection differs from the database; SQL was not executed"
                notify(item.status, vim.log.levels.WARN)
                M.refresh_view(state)
                if state.inline then
                    notify("Set missing parameter values in Query Mode")
                else
                    M.draw_parameters(state, true)
                end
                return
            end
            params = bound
            execute()
        end)
        M.refresh_view(state)
    end
    
    function M.cancel()
        local state = run_state()
        local item = console(state)
        if item and item.client and item.client.busy then
            item.auto_pending = nil
            item.auto_revision = (item.auto_revision or 0) + 1
            item.status = "cancellation requested; reconnect may be required"
            item.client:cancel()
            M.refresh_view(state)
        end
    end
    
    # FoxQuery worker
    
    The Lua editor uses this Go worker over UTF-8 JSON lines on stdin/stdout. SQL,
    parameters, passwords, and full execution errors stay in memory. The bundled
    executables include their runtime and drivers; no external database CLI,
    SQLite library, or driver installation is required.
    
    `go.mod`/`go.sum` pin source dependency versions. The build scripts generate
    `vendor/` locally when missing, including dependency licenses, then reuse it.
    Vendor source is Git-ignored to keep clones small. SQLite is embedded using
    modernc.org/sqlite. MySQL/MariaDB use go-sql-driver/mysql and PostgreSQL uses pgx. MariaDB profiles
    select `engine: "mysql"` and use the MySQL SQL dialect.
    Go's runtime is compiled into each
    executable with CGO disabled, including Linux builds without a libc dependency.
    
    From this directory, with Go 1.24 or newer installed:
    
    ```powershell
    .\build.ps1
    ```
    
    ```sh
    bash build.sh
    ```
    
    This runs tests and builds Windows and Linux executables for amd64
    under `../../fox-query/`, then writes `SHA256SUMS`. The first build needs network
    access unless the pinned dependencies are already cached. Later builds reuse
    `vendor/` offline. After changing `go.mod`/`go.sum`, regenerate it with
    `go mod vendor`; Go rejects stale vendor metadata. Only rebuilding requires Go.
    The first Go invocation may initialize its toolchain cache; compiler and module
    caches used by the shared builder are under `.cache/`.
    Intermediate caches, `vendor/`, `dist/`, and generated `tools/fox-query/licenses/`
    notices are ignored; source, module lockfiles, executables, and checksums belong
    in Git. The builder copies those notices locally from dependencies and the Go toolchain.
    On Linux the Lua launcher restores executable permissions if checkout did not
    preserve them. macOS and 32-bit builds are
    not included.
    
    After the first build, run backend tests without rebuilding everything:
    
    ```sh
    go test -mod=vendor ./...
    ```
    
    `tests/query/fixture.db` is a committed SQLite test fixture. To regenerate it
    after changing `fixture()` in `driver_test.go`, set `FOXQUERY_FIXTURE` to that
    file's absolute path and run `go test -mod=vendor -run TestExportLuaFixture`.
    Production code has no database creation or write operation.
    
    The worker preserves the read-only guard, driver-bound `?` parameters, schema
    metadata, 30-second deadline, cancellation, 1,000-row/8-MiB result caps, and
    20,000-row metadata cap. SQLite also opens existing files read-only, enables
    query-only mode, and disables attachments. MySQL/PostgreSQL use read-only
    sessions. A capped MySQL stream is canceled and disconnected to avoid draining
    unbounded results. Use restricted server credentials as before.
    
    Profiles, saved queries, metadata caches, and local passwords keep their existing
    formats. No user databases or project-private files are migrated or deleted.
    
    Server connections still require network access and valid credentials; custom
    CA files must exist on the destination PC. The portability guarantee here is
    for FoxQuery, not every optional FoxVim language server or plugin.
    ✓ • 96ms

    package main
    
    import (
        "context"
        "crypto/tls"
        "crypto/x509"
        "database/sql"
        "encoding/base64"
        "encoding/json"
        "fmt"
        "net"
        "net/url"

        "strconv"

        "github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/stdlib"
        "modernc.org/sqlite"
        sqlite3 "modernc.org/sqlite/lib"
    )
    
    type profile struct {
        Engine      string   `json:"engine"`
        Path        string   `json:"path"`
        Host        string   `json:"host"`
        Port        int      `json:"port"`
        Database    string   `json:"database"`
        User        string   `json:"user"`
        Password    string   `json:"password"`
        Schemas     []string `json:"schemas"`
        Schema      string   `json:"schema"`
        SSLMode     string   `json:"sslmode"`
        SSLRootCert string   `json:"sslrootcert"`
        SSLCA       string   `json:"ssl_ca"`
    }
    type driver struct {
        db      *sql.DB
        conn    *sql.Conn
        profile profile
        syntax  string
    }
    
    func (d *driver) close() {
        if d.conn != nil {
            d.conn.Close()
            d.conn = nil
        }
        if d.db != nil {
            d.db.Close()
            d.db = nil
        }
    }
    func (d *driver) connect(ctx context.Context, p profile, root string) (any, error) {
        d.close()
        d.profile = p
        d.syntax = p.Engine
        var db *sql.DB
        var err error
        switch p.Engine {
        case "sqlite":
            path := p.Path
            if !filepath.IsAbs(path) {
                path = filepath.Join(root, path)
            }
            path, err = filepath.Abs(path)
            if err != nil {
                return nil, err
            }
            info, e := os.Stat(path)
            if e != nil || !info.Mode().IsRegular() {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("SQLite path must be an existing file")
            }
            path = filepath.ToSlash(path)
            if !strings.HasPrefix(path, "/") {
                path = "/" + path
            }
            u := url.URL{Scheme: "file", Path: path}
            db, err = sql.Open("sqlite", u.String()+"?mode=ro&_pragma=query_only(1)")
        case "mysql":
            cfg := mysql.NewConfig()
            cfg.User = p.User
            cfg.Passwd = p.Password
            cfg.DBName = p.Database
            cfg.Net = "tcp"
            host := p.Host
            if host == "" {
                host = "127.0.0.1"
            }
            port := p.Port
            if port == 0 {
                port = 3306
            }
            cfg.Addr = net.JoinHostPort(host, strconv.Itoa(port))
            cfg.Timeout = 10 * time.Second
            cfg.ReadTimeout = 30 * time.Second
            cfg.WriteTimeout = 10 * time.Second
            cfg.Params = map[string]string{"charset": "utf8mb4"}
            if p.SSLCA != "" {
                pem, e := os.ReadFile(p.SSLCA)
                if e != nil {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Cannot read MySQL CA certificate")
    // FoxQuery JSON-lines worker. Credentials and SQL arrive only over stdin.
    package main
    
    import (
        "bufio"
        "bytes"
        "context"
        "encoding/json"
        "fmt"
        "io"
        "os"
        "sync"
        "time"
    )
    
    type request struct {
        ID      any     `json:"id"`
        Op      string  `json:"op"`
        Profile profile `json:"profile"`
        Root    string  `json:"root"`
        SQL     string  `json:"sql"`
        Params  []any   `json:"params"`
    }
    
    func serve(input io.Reader, output io.Writer) {
        var lock sync.Mutex
        enc := json.NewEncoder(output)
        emit := func(v any) { lock.Lock(); defer lock.Unlock(); _ = enc.Encode(v) }
        var cancelLock sync.Mutex
        var current context.CancelFunc
        cancel := func() {
            cancelLock.Lock()
            defer cancelLock.Unlock()
            if current != nil {
                current()
            }
        }
        requests := make(chan request)
        started := make(chan struct{})
        done := make(chan struct{})
        d := &driver{}
        go func() {
            defer close(done)
            defer d.close()
            for r := range requests {
                ctx, stop := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
                cancelLock.Lock()
                current = stop
                cancelLock.Unlock()
                started <- struct{}{}
                var result any
                var err error
                switch {
                case r.Op == "connect":
                    result, err = d.connect(ctx, r.Profile, r.Root)
                case r.Op == "disconnect":
                    d.close()
                    result = object{}
                case d.conn == nil:
                    err = fmt.Errorf("Connect first")
                case r.Op == "metadata":
                    result, err = d.metadata(ctx)
                case r.Op == "prepare":
                    var ss []statement
                    ss, err = prepare(r.SQL, d.syntax)
                    n := 0
                    for _, s := range ss {
                        n += len(s.positions)
                    }
                    result = object{"parameters": n, "statements": len(ss)}
                case r.Op == "execute":
                    result, err = d.execute(ctx, r.SQL, r.Params, emit, r.ID)
                default:
                    err = fmt.Errorf("Unknown driver operation")
                }
                canceled := ctx.Err() != nil
                if canceled && err == nil {
                    err = ctx.Err()
                }
                cancelLock.Lock()
                current = nil
                cancelLock.Unlock()
                stop()
                if err != nil {
                    safe := "Database operation failed; check SQL, connection, permissions, and server compatibility"
                    if r.Op == "prepare" || r.Op == "connect" {
                        safe = err.Error()
                    }
                    if canceled {
                        d.close()
                        safe = "Canceled or timed out; reconnect before running SQL"
                    }
                    response := object{"id": r.ID, "error": safe, "disconnected": d.conn == nil}
                    if r.Op == "execute" {
                        response["error_detail"] = err.Error()
                    }
                    emit(response)
                } else {
                    emit(object{"id": r.ID, "result": result})
                }
            }
        }()
        scanner := bufio.NewScanner(input)
        scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 4096), 2*1024*1024)
        for scanner.Scan() {
            var r request
            dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(scanner.Bytes()))
            dec.UseNumber()
            if err := dec.Decode(&r); err != nil {
                emit(object{"id": r.ID, "error": "Invalid driver request"})
                continue
            }
            if r.Op == "cancel" {
                cancel()
            } else {
                requests <- r
                <-started
            }
        }
        if scanner.Err() != nil {
            emit(object{"error": "Driver request is too large or unreadable"})
        }
        cancel()
        close(requests)
        <-done
    }
    func main() { serve(os.Stdin, os.Stdout) }

    package main
    
    import (

    func fixture(t *testing.T) string {
        t.Helper()
        path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "fixture.db")
        db, err := sql.Open("sqlite", path)
        if err != nil {
            t.Fatal(err)
        }
        _, err = db.Exec(`CREATE TABLE customers(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,name TEXT); CREATE TABLE orders(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,customer_id INTEGER REFERENCES customers,total TEXT); INSERT INTO customers VALUES(1,'Ada'); INSERT INTO orders VALUES(10,1,'12345678901234567890.12'); CREATE TABLE things(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,name TEXT); INSERT INTO things VALUES(1,'fox');`)
        if err != nil {
            t.Fatal(err)
        }
        if err = db.Close(); err != nil {
            t.Fatal(err)
        }
        return path
    }
    func connected(t *testing.T) *driver {
        t.Helper()
        d := &driver{}
        _, err := d.connect(context.Background(), profile{Engine: "sqlite", Path: fixture(t)}, "")
        if err != nil {
            t.Fatal(err)
        }
        t.Cleanup(d.close)
        return d
    }
    func execute(t *testing.T, d *driver, s string, p ...any) object {
        t.Helper()
        r, e := d.execute(context.Background(), s, p, func(any) {}, 1)
        if e != nil {
            t.Fatal(e)
        }
        return r.(map[string]any)
    }
    func TestSQLite(t *testing.T) {
        d := connected(t)
        m, e := d.metadata(context.Background())
        if e != nil {
            t.Fatal(e)
        }
        rels := m.(map[string]any)["relations"].([]object)
        if len(rels) != 1 || rels[0]["columns"].([]object)[0]["target"] != "id" {
            t.Fatal(m)
        }
        r := execute(t, d, "SELECT ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 9007199254740993, 1.25, x'00ff'", "'; DROP TABLE customers; --", nil, "", "á🦊獣", json.Number("9007199254740993"))["sets"].([]resultSet)[0]
        if r.Rows[0][4] != "9007199254740993" || r.Rows[0][5] != "9007199254740993" || r.Rows[0][1] != nil || !r.Strings[0][0] || r.Strings[0][5] || r.Rows[0][7].(map[string]any)["base64"] != "AP8=" {
            t.Fatal(r)
        }
        if execute(t, d, "SELECT total FROM orders")["sets"].([]resultSet)[0].Rows[0][0] != "12345678901234567890.12" {
            t.Fatal("precision lost")
        }
        if _, e = d.conn.ExecContext(context.Background(), "DELETE FROM orders"); e == nil {
            t.Fatal("database allowed writes")
        }
        if _, e = d.conn.ExecContext(context.Background(), "ATTACH ':memory:' AS secret"); e == nil {
            t.Fatal("database allowed attachments")
        }
        if _, e = d.execute(context.Background(), "SELECT ?", nil, func(any) {}, 1); e == nil {
            t.Fatal("missing parameter allowed")
        }
    }
    func TestMissingFile(t *testing.T) {
        path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "missing.db")
        d := &driver{}
        defer d.close()
        if _, e := d.connect(context.Background(), profile{Engine: "sqlite", Path: path}, ""); e == nil {
            t.Fatal("missing file accepted")
        }
        if _, e := os.Stat(path); !os.IsNotExist(e) {
            t.Fatal("missing database was created")
        }
    }
    func TestBoundsAndCancel(t *testing.T) {
        d := connected(t)
        r := execute(t, d, "WITH RECURSIVE n(x) AS (SELECT 1 UNION ALL SELECT x+1 FROM n WHERE x<5000) SELECT x FROM n")["sets"].([]resultSet)[0]
        if len(r.Rows) != 1000 || !r.Truncated {
            t.Fatal(r)
        }
        r = execute(t, d, "SELECT printf('%.*c',9000000,'x')")["sets"].([]resultSet)[0]
        if len(r.Rows) != 0 || !r.Truncated {
            t.Fatal("byte cap failed")
        }
        ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 20*time.Millisecond)
        defer cancel()
        _, err := d.execute(ctx, "WITH RECURSIVE n(x) AS (SELECT 1 UNION ALL SELECT x+1 FROM n WHERE x<100000000) SELECT sum(x) FROM n", nil, func(any) {}, 1)
        if err == nil {
            t.Fatal("query was not canceled")
        }
    }
    func TestSQL(t *testing.T) {
        if _, err := prepare("SELECT 1 /* /* */ INTO OUTFILE 'x' -- */", "mysql"); err == nil {
            t.Fatal("nested comments bypassed the write guard")
        }
        if _, err := prepare("SELECT 1 /* outer /* inner */ outer */", "postgres"); err != nil {
            t.Fatal(err)
        }
        for _, s := range []string{"DELETE FROM customers", "WITH x AS (SELECT 1) DELETE FROM orders", "SELECT 1 INTO OUTFILE 'x'", "/*! SELECT 1 */", "ATTACH 'x' AS x", "PRAGMA query_only=OFF", "SELECT 'unfinished", "SELECT (1", "SELECT ?1"} {
            if _, e := prepare(s, "mysql"); e == nil {
                t.Fatal("accepted", s)
            }
        }
        ss, e := prepare("SELECT ';?' AS literal; -- ?\nSELECT ?", "sqlite")
        if e != nil || len(ss) != 2 || len(ss[1].positions) != 1 {
            t.Fatal(ss, e)
        }
        ss, e = prepare("SELECT doc ? 'name', doc ?| array['x'], ? FROM things WHERE id = ?", "postgres")
        if e != nil || len(ss[0].positions) != 2 || !strings.Contains(boundSQL(ss[0], "postgres"), "$2") {
            t.Fatal(ss, e)
        }
        ss, e = prepare("SELECT $$?;$$, ?", "postgres")
        if e != nil || len(ss[0].positions) != 1 {
            t.Fatal(ss, e)
        }
        ss, e = prepare("SELECT '50%', ?, 100 % 7", "mysql")
        if e != nil || boundSQL(ss[0], "mysql") != ss[0].sql {
            t.Fatal(ss, e)
        }
        if _, e = prepare("SELECT 1 AS \"(\"", "sqlite"); e != nil {
            t.Fatal(e)
        }
    }

    -- Keep the Query tab and its floating windows alive when changing environments.
    -- Reopening floats would invalidate their buffer-local callbacks and pending choices.
    function M.leave_environment()
        local root = storage.root()
        local state = M.states[root]
        local current_win = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
        local view = inline.current()
        local resume = {
            root = root,
            popups = {},
            focus = current_win,
            inline_focus = view and view.result_win == current_win and view or nil,
        }
        if state and valid(state.tab, "tab") then
            if vim.api.nvim_get_current_tabpage() == state.tab then
                resume.query = state
                state.mode, state.parked = "editor", true
                M.privacy_leave(state)
                if valid(state.editor_tab, "tab") then
                    vim.api.nvim_set_current_tabpage(state.editor_tab)
                else
                    vim.cmd.tabnew()
                    state.editor_tab = vim.api.nvim_get_current_tabpage()
                end
            else
                state.parked = true
            end
        end
        for _, win in ipairs(vim.api.nvim_tabpage_list_wins(0)) do
            local config = vim.api.nvim_win_get_config(win)
            if config.relative ~= "" and not config.hide and vim.b[vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(win)].fox_query_root == root then
                if config.relative == "editor" then
                    resume.popups[#resume.popups + 1] = win
                    vim.api.nvim_win_set_config(win, { hide = true })
                end
            end
        end
        resume.inline = inline.leave_environment(root)
        return resume
    end
    
    function M.enter_environment(resume)
        if not resume or resume.root ~= storage.root() then
            return
        end
        inline.enter_environment(resume.inline)
        for _, win in ipairs(resume.popups) do
            if valid(win, "win") and vim.api.nvim_win_get_tabpage(win) == vim.api.nvim_get_current_tabpage() then
                vim.api.nvim_win_set_config(win, { hide = false })
            end
        end
        if resume.query and valid(resume.query.tab, "tab") then
            M.show(resume.query.view)
        end
        local win = resume.inline_focus and resume.inline_focus.result_win or resume.focus
        if valid(win, "win") and vim.api.nvim_win_get_tabpage(win) == vim.api.nvim_get_current_tabpage() then
            vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(win)
        end
    end
    
    function M.suspend()
        local restore, buffers = {}, {}
        for _, state in pairs(M.states) do
            if valid(state.tab, "tab") then
                restore[#restore + 1] = state
                M.hide(state)
            end
        end
        for _, buf in ipairs(vim.api.nvim_list_bufs()) do
            if vim.b[buf].fox_query_root then
                buffers[#buffers + 1] = { buf, vim.bo[buf].buflisted, vim.bo[buf].buftype }
                vim.bo[buf].buflisted, vim.bo[buf].buftype = false, "nofile"
            end
        end
        return function(reopen)
            for _, saved in ipairs(buffers) do
                if valid(saved[1], "buf") then
                    vim.bo[saved[1]].buflisted, vim.bo[saved[1]].buftype = saved[2], saved[3]
                end
            end
            connection_status = workspace.connection_status,
            metadata = {},
        }
        views[win] = view
        return view
    end
    
    function M.detach(win)
        local view = views[win]
        if not view then
            return
        end
        views[win] = nil
        hide(view)
        ui.close(view.result_popup)
        if view.result_buf and vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(view.result_buf) then
            vim.api.nvim_buf_delete(view.result_buf, { force = true })
        end
    end
    
    function M.leave_environment(root)
        local resume = {}
        for win, view in pairs(views) do
            if view.root == root and shown(view) then
                resume[#resume + 1] = view
                hide(view)
                local placeholder = vim.api.nvim_create_buf(false, false)
                vim.bo[placeholder].bufhidden = "wipe"
                vim.api.nvim_win_set_buf(win, placeholder)
            end
        end
        return resume
    end
    
    function M.enter_environment(resume)
        for _, view in ipairs(resume or {}) do
            if views[view.sql_win] == view and attached(view) then
                vim.api.nvim_win_set_buf(view.sql_win, view.active)
                M.refresh(view)
            end
        end
    end
    
    function M.close_root(root)
        for win, view in pairs(views) do
            if view.root == root then
                M.detach(win)
            end
        end
    end
    
    function M.update()
        for win, view in pairs(views) do
            if not attached(view) then
                M.detach(win)
            else
                M.refresh(view)
            end
        end
    end

    function M.autorun(state)
        state = state or M.state()
        if
            not state.autorun
            or state.mode ~= "query"
            or state.root ~= storage.root()
            or not valid(state.tab, "tab")
            or vim.api.nvim_get_current_tabpage() ~= state.tab
        then
            return
        end
        local item = console(state)
        if not item or not item.client or not item.client.connected then
            return
        end
        local text, offset = text_at_cursor(state)
        if #text > 128 * 1024 then
            return
        end
        local engine = require("fox.langs.sql.dialect").read(state.active)
            or vim.b[state.active].fox_sql_dialect
            or item.syntax
            or item.profile.engine
        local statement = sql.scope(text, offset, "statement", nil, engine)
        if not statement then
            return
        end
        local tokens, incomplete = sql.tokens(statement, engine)
        if incomplete or not tokens[1] or tokens[1].kind ~= "word" or tokens[1].upper ~= "SELECT" then
            return
        end
        for _, edge in ipairs(sql.analyze(statement, item.metadata or state.metadata).edges) do
            if edge.kind:upper() ~= "CROSS" and (not edge.predicate or vim.trim(edge.predicate) == "") then
                return
            end
        end
        if item.auto_sql == statement and item.auto_connection == item.client then
            return
        end
        if item.client.busy then
            item.auto_pending = true
            return
        end
            }

    // Shared builder for build.ps1 and build.sh.

    func main() {
        _, file, _, ok := runtime.Caller(0)
        if !ok {
            panic("Cannot find build directory")
        }
        root := filepath.Dir(file)
        if err := os.Chdir(root); err != nil {
            panic(err)
        }
        env := append(os.Environ(), "CGO_ENABLED=0", "GOTELEMETRY=off", "GOCACHE="+filepath.Join(root, ".cache", "build"), "GOPATH="+filepath.Join(root, ".cache", "go"))
        run := func(args []string, extra ...string) {
            cmd := exec.Command("go", args...)
            cmd.Env = append(env, extra...)
            cmd.Stdout = os.Stdout
            cmd.Stderr = os.Stderr
            if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
                panic(err)
            }
        }
        if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(root, "vendor", "modules.txt")); os.IsNotExist(err) {
            fmt.Println("Generating vendor dependencies from go.mod/go.sum...")
            run([]string{"mod", "vendor"})
        } else if err != nil {
            panic(err)
        } else {
            fmt.Println("Reusing vendor dependencies")
        }
        env = append(env, "GOPROXY=off", "GOSUMDB=off")
        run([]string{"test", "-mod=vendor", "./..."})
        output := filepath.Clean(filepath.Join(root, "..", "..", "fox-query"))
        if err := os.MkdirAll(output, 0755); err != nil {
            panic(err)
        }
        vendor := filepath.Join(root, "vendor")
        if err := filepath.WalkDir(vendor, func(path string, entry os.DirEntry, err error) error {
            if err != nil {
                return err
            }
            if entry.IsDir() || entry.Name() != "LICENSE" {
                return nil
            }
            relative, err := filepath.Rel(vendor, path)
            if err != nil {
                return err
            }
            destination := filepath.Join(output, "licenses", relative)
            if err = os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(destination), 0755); err != nil {
                return err
            }
            data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
            if err != nil {
                return err
            }
            return os.WriteFile(destination, data, 0644)
        }); err != nil {
            panic(err)
        }
        license, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(runtime.GOROOT(), "LICENSE"))
        // Arch Linux packages install the license outside GOROOT.
        if os.IsNotExist(err) && runtime.GOOS == "linux" {
            license, err = os.ReadFile("/usr/share/licenses/go/LICENSE")
        }
        if err != nil {
            panic(err)
        }
        if err = os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(output, "licenses", "Go-LICENSE"), license, 0644); err != nil {
            panic(err)
        }
        checksums := ""

                name += ".exe"
            }
            path := filepath.Join(output, name)
            run([]string{"build", "-mod=vendor", "-trimpath", "-buildvcs=false", "-ldflags=-s -w -buildid=", "-o", path, "."}, "GOOS="+platform.os, "GOARCH="+platform.arch)
            b, err := os.ReadFile(path)
            if err != nil {
                panic(err)
            }
            checksums += fmt.Sprintf("%x  %s\n", sha256.Sum256(b), name)
            fmt.Println("Built", name)
        }
        if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(output, "SHA256SUMS"), []byte(checksums), 0644); err != nil {
            panic(err)
        }

## Assistant

    local t = require("fox.lib.foxnvim.test")
    local query = require("fox.query")
    local envs = require("plugins.fox.tools.environments")

    dofile(vim.fn.stdpath("config") .. "/tests/query/neotree.lua")
    
    t.describe("Query environment restoration", function()
        t.it("restores editor focus and Query panels through repeated switches", function()
            local root, cwd = vim.fn.tempname(), vim.fn.getcwd()
            local original_win, original_buf = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win(), vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf()
            local saved_envs, saved_slot = _G._fox_environments, _G._fox_active_env_slot
            local saved_layout = require("fox.query.layout").path
            local foreign = vim.api.nvim_create_buf(true, false)
            vim.api.nvim_buf_set_name(foreign, root .. "/previous-project/old.lua")
            vim.fn.mkdir(root .. "/one", "p")
            vim.fn.mkdir(root .. "/two", "p")
            require("fox.query.layout").path = root .. "/layout.json"
            _G._fox_environments, _G._fox_active_env_slot = {}, 1
            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                vim.api.nvim_set_current_dir(root .. "/one")
                envs.create_environment(1, root .. "/one", "One", false)
                envs.create_environment(2, root .. "/two", "Two", false)
                vim.cmd("enew")
                local editor = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
                vim.cmd("Neotree focus dir=" .. vim.fn.fnameescape(root .. "/one"))
                vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(editor)
                envs.switch_environment(2)
                envs.switch_environment(1)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()).toBe(editor)
                query.setup()
                for _, view in ipairs({ "standard", "visual" }) do
                    query.show(view)
                    local state = query.state()
                    local buf = state.active
                    vim.api.nvim_buf_set_lines(buf, 0, -1, false, { "SELECT 42;" })
                    state.consoles[buf].result = { sets = { { columns = { "answer" }, rows = { { "42" } } } } }
                    query.refresh_view(state)
                    for _ = 1, 3 do
                        envs.switch_environment(2)
                        envs.switch_environment(1)
                        vim.wait(200, function()
                            return false
                        end, 10)
                        t.expect(vim.bo[foreign].buflisted).toBe(false)
                        t.expect(vim.b[foreign].fox_env_slot).toBe(nil)
                        t.expect(envs.is_buffer_in_current_environment(foreign)).toBe(false)
                        t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_tabpage()).toBe(state.tab)
                        t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(state.sql_win)).toBe(buf)
                        t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(state.result_win)).toBe(state.result_buf)
                        t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(state.side_win)).toBe(state.side_buf)
                        t.expect(table.concat(vim.api.nvim_buf_get_lines(state.result_buf, 0, -1, false), "\n")).toContain("42")
                        for _, win in ipairs(vim.api.nvim_tabpage_list_wins(state.tab)) do
                            local pane = vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(win)
                            if vim.bo[pane].filetype == "neo-tree" then
                                t.expect(vim.b[pane].neo_tree_source).toBe("fox_query")
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(state.result_win)).toBe(state.result_buf)
                    t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(state.sql_win)).toBe(buf)
                    t.expect(vim.bo[buf].modified).toBeTruthy()
                    query.hide()
                    t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()).toBe(editor)
                    t.expect(envs.get_environment(1).neotree_open).toBeTruthy()
                end
            end)
            for _, name in ipairs({ "one", "two" }) do
                local state = query.states[storage.root(root .. "/" .. name)]
                if state then
                    for buf in pairs(state.consoles) do
                        vim.bo[buf].modified = false
                    end
                    query.close_root(state.root)
                end
            end
            vim.cmd("silent! Neotree close")
            if vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(original_win) then
                vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(original_win)
            end
            if vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(original_buf) then
                vim.api.nvim_set_current_buf(original_buf)
            end
            vim.api.nvim_buf_delete(foreign, { force = true })
            for _, buf in ipairs(vim.api.nvim_list_bufs()) do
                local owner = vim.b[buf].fox_query_root
                if owner == storage.root(root .. "/one") or owner == storage.root(root .. "/two") then
                    vim.api.nvim_buf_delete(buf, { force = true })
                end
            end
            _G._fox_environments, _G._fox_active_env_slot = saved_envs, saved_slot
            require("fox.query.layout").path = saved_layout
            vim.api.nvim_set_current_dir(cwd)
            vim.fn.delete(root, "rf")
            if not ok then
                error(err)
            end
        end)
