    local M = {}
    local views = {}
    local ui = require("fox.core.ui")
    local render = require("fox.query.render")
    
    local function attached(view)
        return vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(view.sql_win)
            and vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(view.active)
            and view.consoles[view.active] ~= nil
    end
    
    local function shown(view)
        return attached(view)
            and vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(view.sql_win) == view.active
            and vim.api.nvim_win_get_tabpage(view.sql_win) == vim.api.nvim_get_current_tabpage()
    end
    
    function M.current()
        local win = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
        local view = views[win]
        if view and shown(view) then
            return view
        end
        for _, candidate in pairs(views) do
            if (candidate.result_win == win or candidate.result_popup == win) and shown(candidate) then
                return candidate
            end
        end
    end
    
    function M.owns(win, buf)
        return views[win] ~= nil and views[win].active == buf and attached(views[win])
    end
    
    local function hide(view)
        local win = view.result_win
        view.result_win = nil
        ui.close(win)
    end
    
    function M.refresh(view)
        local item = view.consoles[view.active]
        if not shown(view) then
            hide(view)
            return
        end
        if not item.result then
            hide(view)
            ui.close(view.result_popup)
            view.result_popup, view.result_seen = nil, nil
            return
        end
        if view.result_seen ~= item.result then
            view.result_seen, view.results_hidden = item.result, false
            item.result_lines, item.result_rows, item.result_headers = render.results(item.result)
            local lines = vim.list_extend({}, item.result_lines)
            vim.list_extend(lines, {
                "  QUERY / RESULTS  |  " .. (item.result_label or item.status),
                "  [Shift+H/J/K/L] Move cell   [Ctrl+K] Back to SQL   [q] Hide",
            })
            if not view.result_buf or not vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(view.result_buf) then
                view.result_buf = ui.scratch_buffer({ filetype = "foxquery" })
                vim.bo[view.result_buf].bufhidden = "hide"
                vim.b[view.result_buf].fox_query_root = view.root
                view.bind_result_cells(view.result_buf)
                vim.keymap.set({ "n", "i", "v" }, "<C-k>", function()
                    if vim.api.nvim_get_current_win() == view.result_popup then
                        ui.close(view.result_popup)
                        view.result_popup = nil
                    end
                    if shown(view) then
                        vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(view.sql_win)
                    end
                end, { buffer = view.result_buf, silent = true, desc = "Query: focus SQL" })
                for _, key in ipairs(ui.close_keys) do
                    vim.keymap.set("n", key, function()
                        if vim.api.nvim_get_current_win() == view.result_popup then
                            ui.close(view.result_popup)
                            view.result_popup = nil
                        else
                            view.results_hidden = true
                            hide(view)
                        end
                        if shown(view) then
                            vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(view.sql_win)
                        end
                    end, { buffer = view.result_buf, silent = true })
                end
            end
            vim.bo[view.result_buf].modifiable = true
            vim.api.nvim_buf_set_lines(view.result_buf, 0, -1, false, ui.sanitize_lines(lines))
            vim.bo[view.result_buf].modifiable = false
        end
        local height = vim.api.nvim_win_get_height(view.sql_win)
        if view.results_hidden or height < 4 then
            hide(view)
            return
        end
        local config = {
            relative = "win",
            win = view.sql_win,
            anchor = "SW",
            row = height + (vim.wo[view.sql_win].winbar ~= "" and 1 or 0),
            col = 0,
            width = vim.api.nvim_win_get_width(view.sql_win),
            height = math.min(12, math.max(2, math.floor(height / 3))),
            style = "minimal",
            border = "none",
            focusable = false,
            mouse = false,
            zindex = 45,
        }
        if view.result_win and vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(view.result_win) then
            vim.api.nvim_win_set_config(view.result_win, config)
        else
            view.result_win = vim.api.nvim_open_win(view.result_buf, false, config)
            vim.wo[view.result_win].wrap = false
        end
    end
    
    function M.popup(view)
        if not shown(view) then
            return
        end
        M.refresh(view)
        if not view.result_buf or not vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(view.result_buf) then
            return
        end
        ui.close(view.result_popup)
        local _, win = ui.float(view.result_buf, { width = 0.9, height = 0.9, title = " Query results | q / Esc close " })
        view.result_popup = win
        vim.wo[win].wrap = false
    end
    
    function M.focus_results(view)
        view.results_hidden = false
        M.refresh(view)
        if view.result_win then
            vim.cmd.stopinsert()
            vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(view.result_win)
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

            inline = true,
            workspace = workspace,
            root = workspace.root,

    function M.run(kind, supplied_sql, supplied_params, source_table, automatic, popup, state)
        if kind and not vim.tbl_contains({ "statement", "selection", "section", "file" }, kind) then
            notify("Choose statement, selection, section, or file", vim.log.levels.WARN)
            return
        end
        state = run_state(state)
        local item, buf = console(state)
        local connected = item and item.client and item.client.connected
        if not connected and automatic then
            return
        end
        local profile = item and connection_profile(state, item)
        if not profile then
            M.profile()
            notify("Select a connection and press Enter, then run this SQL again")
            return
        end
        if connected and item.client.busy then
            notify("Console is busy; cancel or wait", vim.log.levels.WARN)
            return
        end
        local text, offset = text_at_cursor(state)
        local selection
        if kind == "selection" then
            if vim.fn.visualmode() == "\22" then
                notify("Block selections are unsupported", vim.log.levels.WARN)
                return
            end
            local first, last = vim.api.nvim_buf_get_mark(buf, "<"), vim.api.nvim_buf_get_mark(buf, ">")
            if first[1] == 0 or last[1] == 0 then
                notify("Select SQL first", vim.log.levels.WARN)

    $ cat docs/terminals.md; sed -n '1,220p' lua/plugins/fox/dev/terminal.lua; sed -n '285,570p' lua/plugins/fox/dev/terminal.lua; rg -n 'terminal' lua/plugins/fox/tools/environments.lua | head -70
    # 🖥️ Multi-Terminal Manager (`plugins.fox.dev.terminal`)
    
    [← Back to Wiki Index](index.md)
    
    The **Lazy-Loading Multi-Terminal Manager** manages up to 9 independent terminal buffers with instant switching, split height persistence, and clean window focus navigation.
    
    Clicking terminal content enters terminal input mode, including when the terminal already has focus. Borders and separators remain available for resizing.
    
    ---
    
    ## ⚡ Key Capabilities
    
    1. **9 Independent Buffers**: Switch between terminals 1 through 9 instantly using `<Alt + 1..9>`.
    2. **Toggle Key**: Press `<Ctrl + ;>` to toggle open or hidden state for the currently selected terminal.
    3. **Floating & Docked Layouts**: Toggle between Docked Split (bottom, default on fresh install) and Floating Overlay ("on top", centered popup or bottom overlay) so the buffers and dashboard above do not resize or shift.
    4. **Height & Layout Persistence**: Remembers mouse-dragged split height (`terminal_height`) and layout selection (`terminal_layout`).
    5. **WSL Interop**: Automatically spawns terminal in WSL when working inside a WSL filesystem path.
    6. **Return Focus**: Hiding the terminal returns to the window used to enter it, including Git Center, even when the terminal was already visible.
    
    ---
    
    ## ⌨️ Terminal Shortcuts & Commands
    
    - `<Alt + 1..9>`: Select & switch to terminal #1..9 (Normal, Insert, and Terminal modes)
    - `<Ctrl + ;>`: Toggle show/hide for selected terminal
    - `<Ctrl + Up>`: Stretch terminal bigger (stretching upwards from top edge in bottom float / docked split; centered expansion in center float)
    - `<Ctrl + Down>`: Shrink terminal smaller (anchored at bottom edge in bottom float / docked split; centered shrink in center float)
    - `<C-w>` (inside terminal): Standard Neovim window navigation prefix
    - `<Ctrl + Space>`: Navigate terminal output with Vim motions. `Ctrl+u` / `Ctrl+d` also enter navigation directly and scroll half a page up / down. Use `v` to select, `y` to copy to the system clipboard, and `i` to return to terminal input. These keys no longer reach the shell or terminal application as input. This also works with the text currently visible in terminal applications such as Codex; their own screen and history behavior may limit older content.
    - `:TerminalKillCurrent`: Stop and remove only the visible terminal; hidden slots keep running. Also available as **Kill Current Terminal** in the Command Palette. Does nothing when no terminal is open.
    - `:TerminalToggle`: Toggle show/hide for selected terminal
    - `:TerminalToggleLayout`: Toggle between Docked Split and Floating Overlay
    - `:TerminalLayout [dock|float|bottom_float]`: Set or switch terminal layout mode
    - `:TerminalIncreaseHeight`: Stretch terminal bigger (`<Ctrl+Up>`)
    - `:TerminalDecreaseHeight`: Shrink terminal smaller (`<Ctrl+Down>`)
    - **Command Palette (`<Ctrl+Shift+P>`)**: Search `Terminal` for instant toggle, layout selection, and resize options
    
    ---
    
    ## 💾 Global Persistence (State Directory)
    
    Terminal preferences are persisted globally in Neovim's state directory (`stdpath("state")`), preserving your environment across sessions without modifying project `.foxnvim/*.json` files:
    - Layout choice: `terminal_layout` (`dock`, `float`, or `bottom_float`)
    - Docked height: `terminal_height`
    - Bottom floating height: `terminal_bottom_float_height`
    - Centered floating height: `terminal_float_height`
    
    
    ## Fastfetch images in Unix terminals
    
    Neovim prepends `config/scripts/terminal-bin` from this config to its own `PATH` via
    `fox.core.terminal_fastfetch`. The bundled `fastfetch` wrapper finds the real
    Fastfetch executable through `PATH`; it needs no machine-specific binary path.
    Inside Neovim, it uses Fastfetch's Chafa renderer when `--list-features` lists
    `chafa`, otherwise it clears the image source and uses the distro's built-in
    ASCII logo. Fastfetch must be installed separately; the standalone `chafa`
    command is not required. The wrapper does not start Fastfetch automatically.
    
    This covers `:terminal`, Alt + number terminal slots, and shell startup greetings
    that invoke `fastfetch` through `PATH` (including `ryoku-fastfetch`). It leaves
    Fastfetch's global configuration and terminals launched outside Neovim alone.
    Restart Neovim after installing or updating this integration. Keep the wrapper
    executable when copying this config to another Unix system. Native Windows and
    WSL sessions launched from Windows Neovim are not configured by this integration.
    Shell startup files that replace `PATH` must preserve `config/scripts/terminal-bin` for
    the wrapper to be used. Absolute calls to a different Fastfetch binary bypass it.
    -- ============================================================================
    -- FOX PLUGIN: Multi-Terminal -- nine lazily created terminals in one dock slot.
    -- ============================================================================
    -- WHAT IT DOES
    --   1. Manages `M.settings.count` independent terminals, created on first use.
    --   2. <A-1>..<A-9> selects a terminal and shows it in the dock's terminal pane.
    --   3. <C-;> / <A-;> toggles the SELECTED terminal open and hidden.
    --   4. Remembers the code window you came from, so closing returns focus there.
    --   5. Survives a config reload: the terminal table lives on `_G`, and
    --      `sync_terminals` re-adopts terminal buffers that lost their slot.
    --
    -- WHY BUFFER VARIABLES
    --   `fox_term_num` (slot) and `fox_is_multi_term` (ownership) live on the buffer
    --   itself, so a reload, a session restore or a stray `:terminal` can be mapped
    --   back to a slot without keeping a parallel registry in sync.
    --
    -- COLLABORATORS
    --   fox.core.dock      Bottom dock shared with the task runner.
    --   plugins.fox.tools.wsl    Chooses the WSL shell when the project lives under WSL.
    -- ============================================================================
    
    local lazy_req = require("fox.core.lazy_require")
    local dock = lazy_req("fox.core.dock")
    local store = lazy_req("fox.core.store")
    
    local M = {}
    
    -- ============================================================================
    -- CONFIGURATION
    -- ============================================================================
    
    M.settings = {
        --- How many terminals exist. `<A-n>` is bound for each.
        count = 9,
    
        --- Height of a freshly opened terminal split, when nothing is saved yet.
        default_height = 10,
    
        --- Accepted range for the remembered height; anything else is ignored.
        min_height = 3,
        max_height = 100,
    
        --- Where the last used terminal height is remembered.
        height_file = vim.fn.stdpath("state") .. "/terminal_height",
    
        --- Where the last used centered floating terminal height is remembered.
        float_height_file = vim.fn.stdpath("state") .. "/terminal_float_height",
    
        --- Where the last used bottom floating terminal height is remembered.
        bottom_float_height_file = vim.fn.stdpath("state") .. "/terminal_bottom_float_height",
    
        --- Default layout mode on fresh install: "dock" (bottom split dock).
        --- Can be changed to "float" (centered floating popup) or "bottom_float" (bottom overlay).
        layout = "dock",
    
        --- Where the last used layout preference is remembered across sessions.
        layout_file = vim.fn.stdpath("state") .. "/terminal_layout",
    
        --- Step size in rows for stretching / shrinking terminal height (<C-Up> / <C-Down>).
        resize_step = 2,
    
        --- Floating window dimensions & border options (used when layout is "float" or "bottom_float")
        float = {
            width = 0.85,
            height = 0.80,
            border = "rounded",
        },
    
        keys = {
            --- Prefix for per-terminal selection; the number is appended (`<A-1>`).
            select_prefix = "<A-",
            --- Toggle the selected terminal.
            toggle = { "<C-t>", "<C-T>", "<C-\\>", "<leader>t", "<leader>ft", "<F4>", "<C-;>", "<A-;>" },
            --- Close the terminal window from inside terminal mode.
            close = "<C-w>",
            --- Stretch terminal bigger (<C-Up>).
            resize_up = { "<C-Up>", "<C-S-Up>" },
            --- Shrink terminal smaller (<C-Down>).
            resize_down = { "<C-Down>", "<C-S-Down>" },
            --- Clipboard bridges, because a terminal has no access to registers.
            paste = { "<C-v>", "<C-S-v>" },
            copy = { "<C-c>", "<C-S-c>" },
        },
    }
    
    -- ============================================================================
    -- STATE -- kept on _G so a config reload does not orphan open terminals
    -- ============================================================================
    
    _G._fox_terminals = _G._fox_terminals or {}
    _G._fox_selected_terminal = _G._fox_selected_terminal or 1
    
    --- `[n] = { buf = integer|nil, win = integer|nil }`
    local terminals = _G._fox_terminals
    
    --- Window to return to when the terminal is dismissed.
    local code_win = nil
    
    --- Current terminal split height, persisted across sessions.
    local terminal_height_cached = nil
    
    -- ============================================================================
    -- HEIGHT PERSISTENCE
    -- ============================================================================
    
    --- Reads the remembered height, falling back to the default.
    --- @return integer height
    local function load_saved_height()
        local raw = store.read_file(M.settings.height_file)
        local height = raw and tonumber(raw) or nil
        if height and height >= M.settings.min_height and height <= M.settings.max_height then
            return height
        end
        return M.settings.default_height
    end
    
    local function get_terminal_height()
        if not terminal_height_cached then
            terminal_height_cached = load_saved_height()
        end
        return terminal_height_cached
    end
    
    --- Remembers a new height when it is in range and actually changed.
    --- @param height integer
    local function save_height(height)
        if type(height) ~= "number" or height < M.settings.min_height or height > M.settings.max_height then
            return
        end
        if height == get_terminal_height() then
            return
        end
        terminal_height_cached = height
        store.write_file(M.settings.height_file, tostring(height))
    end
    
    local function load_saved_float_height()
        local raw = store.read_file(M.settings.float_height_file)
        local height = raw and tonumber(vim.trim(raw)) or nil
        if height and height >= M.settings.min_height and height <= M.settings.max_height then
            return height
        end
        local total_h = vim.o.lines or 24
        return math.max(5, math.floor(total_h * (M.settings.float.height or 0.80)))
    end
    
    local function get_float_height()
        if not _G._fox_terminal_float_height then
            _G._fox_terminal_float_height = load_saved_float_height()
        end
        return _G._fox_terminal_float_height
    end
    
    function M.float_size()
        return math.max(
            1,
            math.min(vim.o.columns - 2, math.max(20, math.floor(vim.o.columns * (M.settings.float.width or 0.85))))
        ),
            math.max(1, math.min(vim.o.lines - 4, get_float_height()))
    end
    
    local function save_float_height(height)
        if type(height) ~= "number" or height < M.settings.min_height or height > M.settings.max_height then
            return
        end
        if height == get_float_height() then
            return
        end
        _G._fox_terminal_float_height = height
        store.write_file(M.settings.float_height_file, tostring(height))
    end
    
    local function load_saved_bottom_float_height()
        local raw = store.read_file(M.settings.bottom_float_height_file)
        local height = raw and tonumber(vim.trim(raw)) or nil
        if height and height >= M.settings.min_height and height <= M.settings.max_height then
            return height
        end
        return get_terminal_height()
    end
    
    local function get_bottom_float_height()
        if not _G._fox_terminal_bottom_float_height then
            _G._fox_terminal_bottom_float_height = load_saved_bottom_float_height()
        end
        return _G._fox_terminal_bottom_float_height
    end
    
    local function save_bottom_float_height(height)
        if type(height) ~= "number" or height < M.settings.min_height or height > M.settings.max_height then
            return
        end
        if height == get_bottom_float_height() then
            return
        end
        _G._fox_terminal_bottom_float_height = height
        store.write_file(M.settings.bottom_float_height_file, tostring(height))
    end
    
    -- ============================================================================
    -- LAYOUT PERSISTENCE
    -- ============================================================================
    
    --- Reads the remembered layout ("dock", "float", "bottom_float"), falling back to default ("dock").
    --- @return string layout
    local function load_saved_layout()
        local raw = store.read_file(M.settings.layout_file)
        if raw then
            raw = vim.trim(raw)
        end
        if raw and (raw == "dock" or raw == "float" or raw == "bottom_float") then
            return raw
        end
        return M.settings.layout or "dock"
    end
    
    local function get_terminal_layout()
        if not _G._fox_terminal_layout then
            _G._fox_terminal_layout = load_saved_layout()
        end
                t.win = winid or t.win
                vim.b[bufnr].fox_term_num = n
                vim.b[bufnr].fox_is_multi_term = true
                -- Tag with the active environment slot for isolation
                local env_slot = _G._fox_active_env_slot or 1
                vim.b[bufnr].fox_env_slot = env_slot
                return n
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    
    --- True for a terminal buffer this plugin may own (task outputs are excluded).
    --- Also excludes terminals tagged to a different environment slot.
    --- @param bufnr integer
    --- @return boolean
    local function is_adoptable(bufnr)
        if vim.bo[bufnr].buftype ~= "terminal" or vim.b[bufnr].fox_is_task then
            return false
        end
        -- If the buffer is tagged to a different environment, don't adopt it
        local buf_env_slot = vim.b[bufnr].fox_env_slot
        if buf_env_slot ~= nil then
            local active_env_slot = _G._fox_active_env_slot or 1
            if buf_env_slot ~= active_env_slot then
                return false
            end
        end
        return true
    end
    
    --- Reconciles the slot table with reality: drops dead handles, re-attaches
    --- tagged buffers, and adopts untagged terminals (from a session or `:terminal`).
    --- Respects environment scoping: only syncs terminals belonging to the active environment.
    local function sync_terminals()
        local active_env_slot = _G._fox_active_env_slot or 1
    
        for n = 1, M.settings.count do
            local t = terminals[n]
            if t then
                t.win = is_valid_win(t.win) and t.win or nil
                if is_valid_buf(t.buf) then
                    -- Drop terminals that were re-tagged to a different environment
                    local buf_env_slot = vim.b[t.buf].fox_env_slot
                    if buf_env_slot ~= nil and buf_env_slot ~= active_env_slot then
                        t.buf = nil
                        t.win = nil
                    end
                else
                    t.buf = nil
                end
            end
        end
    
        -- Visible terminals first, so a slot keeps the window it is displayed in.
        for _, winid in ipairs(vim.api.nvim_list_wins()) do
            if vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(winid) then
                local bufnr = vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(winid)
                if vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(bufnr) then
                    -- Skip terminals belonging to other environments
                    local buf_env_slot = vim.b[bufnr].fox_env_slot
                    if buf_env_slot == nil or buf_env_slot == active_env_slot then
                        local term_num = vim.b[bufnr].fox_term_num
                        if term_num and term_num >= 1 and term_num <= M.settings.count then
                            local t = get_term(term_num)
                            t.buf, t.win = bufnr, winid
                        elseif is_adoptable(bufnr) then
                            adopt_buffer(bufnr, winid)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    
        -- Then hidden terminal buffers, which have no window to record.
        for _, bufnr in ipairs(vim.api.nvim_list_bufs()) do
            if vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(bufnr) then
                -- Skip terminals belonging to other environments
                local buf_env_slot = vim.b[bufnr].fox_env_slot
                if buf_env_slot == nil or buf_env_slot == active_env_slot then
                    local term_num = vim.b[bufnr].fox_term_num
                    if term_num and term_num >= 1 and term_num <= M.settings.count then
                        local t = get_term(term_num)
                        if not is_valid_buf(t.buf) then
                            t.buf = bufnr
                        end
                    elseif is_adoptable(bufnr) then
                        adopt_buffer(bufnr, nil)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    
    --- The window currently hosting a terminal: the selected one when it is visible,
    --- otherwise any visible terminal.
    --- @return integer|nil win
    local function get_active_terminal_win()
        sync_terminals()
    
        local selected = terminals[_G._fox_selected_terminal or 1]
        if selected and is_valid_win(selected.win) then
            return selected.win
        end
        for _, t in pairs(terminals) do
            if is_valid_win(t.win) then
                return t.win
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    
    -- ============================================================================
    -- TERMINAL CREATION
    -- ============================================================================
    
    --- `:terminal` command for the current project, routed through WSL when the
    --- project lives on a WSL path.
    --- @return string cmd
    local function terminal_open_cmd()
        local ok, wsl = pcall(require, "plugins.fox.tools.wsl")
        local wsl_cmd = ok and wsl.shell_command_for_cwd(vim.fn.getcwd()) or nil
        if wsl_cmd then
            return "terminal " .. wsl_cmd
        end
        -- Kitty starts the user's interactive shell; use the same initialization
        -- inside Neovim while keeping Bash for POSIX tool/task commands.
        local shell = vim.env.SHELL
        if vim.fn.has("unix") == 1 and shell and vim.fn.executable(shell) == 1 then
            return "terminal " .. vim.fn.fnameescape(shell) .. " -il"
        end
        return "terminal"
    end
    
    --- Shows terminal `n` in `win`, spawning the shell when the slot has no buffer.
    --- @param t table Slot record.
    --- @param n integer Slot number.
    --- @param win integer Window to fill.
    local function fill_window(t, n, win)
        if is_valid_win(win) then
            vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(win)
        end
    
        if is_valid_buf(t.buf) then
            vim.api.nvim_win_set_buf(win, t.buf)
            return
        end
    
        local cwd = vim.fn.getcwd()
        local stat_fn = (vim.uv or vim.loop).fs_stat
        if stat_fn(cwd .. "/.foxnvim/secondary_repos.json") then
            local ok_sec, sec = pcall(require, "fox.git.secondary")
            if ok_sec and sec then
                sec.setup_environment(cwd)
            end
        end
    
        vim.cmd(terminal_open_cmd())
        t.buf = vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf()
        vim.bo[t.buf].buflisted = false
        vim.b[t.buf].fox_term_num = n
        vim.b[t.buf].fox_is_multi_term = true
        vim.b[t.buf].fox_env_slot = _G._fox_active_env_slot or 1
    end
    
    -- ============================================================================
    -- FLOATING WINDOW HELPERS
    -- ============================================================================
    
    --- Checks if a window is a floating window.
    --- @param win integer|nil
    --- @return boolean
    local function is_floating_win(win)
        if not win or type(win) ~= "number" or not vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(win) then
            return false
        end
        local cfg = vim.api.nvim_win_get_config(win)
        return cfg and cfg.relative and cfg.relative ~= ""
    end
    
    --- Opens a floating window for terminal `n`.
    --- @param t table Slot record.
    --- @param n integer Slot number.
    --- @param layout string "float" or "bottom_float".
    --- @return integer win Window handle.
    local function open_floating_window(t, n, layout)
        local z_ok, z_index = pcall(require, "fox.core.z_index")
        local zidx = z_ok and z_index.next_zindex("terminal") or 50
    
        local total_w = vim.o.columns or 80
        local total_h = vim.o.lines or 24
    
        local w, h, r, c
        if layout == "bottom_float" then
            w = math.max(20, total_w - 2)
            h = math.min(total_h - 4, get_bottom_float_height())
            r = math.max(1, total_h - h - 3)
            c = 1
        else
            w, h = M.float_size()
            r = math.max(1, math.floor((total_h - h) / 2) - 1)
            c = math.max(1, math.floor((total_w - w) / 2))
        end
    
        local target_buf = is_valid_buf(t.buf) and t.buf or nil
        local scratch = nil
        if not target_buf then
            scratch = vim.api.nvim_create_buf(false, true)
            target_buf = scratch
        end
    
        local win = vim.api.nvim_open_win(target_buf, true, {
            relative = "editor",
            width = w,
            height = h,
            row = r,
            col = c,
            border = M.settings.float.border or "rounded",
            title = " 🖥️ Terminal #" .. n .. " ",
            title_pos = "center",
            zindex = zidx,
        })
    
        vim.wo[win].number = false
        vim.wo[win].relativenumber = false
        vim.wo[win].signcolumn = "no"
    
        if z_ok and z_index.register then
            z_index.register("terminal", win, { zindex = zidx })
        end
    
        fill_window(t, n, win)
    
        if scratch and is_valid_buf(scratch) and scratch ~= t.buf then
            pcall(vim.api.nvim_buf_delete, scratch, { force = true })
        end
    
        return win
    end
    
    --- Updates the title of a floating terminal window to reflect slot `n`.
    --- @param win integer Window handle.
    --- @param n integer Slot number.
    local function update_float_title(win, n)
        if is_floating_win(win) then
            pcall(vim.api.nvim_win_set_config, win, {
                title = " 🖥️ Terminal #" .. n .. " ",
                title_pos = "center",
            })
        end
    end
    
    -- ============================================================================
    -- PUBLIC API
    -- ============================================================================
    
    --- Currently selected terminal number.
    --- @return integer n
    function M.get_selected_terminal()
        return _G._fox_selected_terminal or 1
    end
    
    local function close_git_center()
        local center = package.loaded["plugins.fox.git.git_center"]
        if center and center.is_open() then
            center.close_git_center({ keep_cached_view = true })
        end
    end
    
    --- Selects terminal `n` and shows it, reusing the visible terminal window.
    --- @param n integer Slot number.

        local active_win = get_active_terminal_win()
    
        if not active_win or current_win ~= active_win then
            code_win = current_win
        end
    
        if is_valid_win(active_win) then
            t.win = active_win

    --- Opens terminal `n`, creating the dock pane or floating window when there is none.
    --- @param n integer|nil Slot number. Defaults to the selected terminal.
    --- @param interactive boolean|nil Enter terminal mode and close Git Center unless false.
    function M.open_terminal(n, interactive)
        if interactive ~= false then
            close_git_center()
        end
        sync_terminals()
    
        n = n or _G._fox_selected_terminal or 1
        _G._fox_selected_terminal = n
    
        local t = get_term(n)
        local current = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
        local active_win = get_active_terminal_win()
    
        if current ~= active_win then
            code_win = current
        end
    
        if not is_valid_win(t.win) then
            if is_valid_win(active_win) then
                t.win = active_win
                fill_window(t, n, t.win)
                update_float_title(t.win, n)
            else
                local layout = get_terminal_layout()
                if layout == "float" or layout == "bottom_float" then
                    t.win = open_floating_window(t, n, layout)
                else
                    t.win = dock.open({ prefer = "terminal", height = get_terminal_height() })
                    fill_window(t, n, t.win)
                    dock.style(t.win)
                end
            end
        end
        vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(t.win)
        if interactive ~= false then
            vim.cmd("startinsert")
        end
    end
    
    --- Hides the visible pane without stopping any terminal job.
    function M.hide_terminal()
        sync_terminals()
        local win = get_active_terminal_win()
        if not win then
            return
        end
        pcall(vim.cmd, "stopinsert")
        vim.api.nvim_win_close(win, true)
        for _, term in pairs(terminals) do
            if term.win == win then
                term.win = nil
            end
        end
    end
    
    --- Stops and removes only the visible terminal, leaving hidden slots running.
    function M.kill_current_terminal()
        local current = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
        local win = is_term_win(current) and current or get_active_terminal_win()
        if not win then
            vim.notify("No terminal is open.", vim.log.levels.INFO, { title = "Terminal" })
            return
        end
        local buf = vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(win)
        pcall(vim.cmd, "stopinsert")
        vim.api.nvim_buf_delete(buf, { force = true })
        if vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(win) then
            pcall(vim.api.nvim_win_close, win, true)
        end
        for _, term in pairs(terminals) do
            if term.buf == buf then
                term.buf, term.win = nil, nil
            end
        end
    end
    
    --- Hides the terminal when focused, shows or creates it otherwise.
    function M.toggle_selected_terminal()
        sync_terminals()
    
        local n = _G._fox_selected_terminal or 1
        local current = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
        local active_win = get_active_terminal_win()
    
        if active_win and current == active_win then
            M.hide_terminal()
    
            if is_valid_win(code_win) then
                pcall(vim.api.nvim_set_current_win, code_win)
            else
                vim.cmd("wincmd p")
            end
            return
        end
    
        M.open_terminal(n)
    end
    
    --- Gets current terminal layout ("dock", "float", or "bottom_float").
    --- @return string
    function M.get_layout()
        return get_terminal_layout()
    end
    
    --- Sets terminal layout mode and shows terminal in the new layout.
    --- @param layout "dock"|"float"|"bottom_float"
    function M.set_layout(layout)
        if layout ~= "dock" and layout ~= "float" and layout ~= "bottom_float" then
            return
        end
        save_layout(layout)
    
        local cur_n = _G._fox_selected_terminal or 1
        local active_win = get_active_terminal_win()
        if active_win and is_valid_win(active_win) then
            pcall(vim.cmd, "stopinsert")
            pcall(vim.api.nvim_win_close, active_win, true)
            for _, term in pairs(terminals) do
                if term.win == active_win then
                    term.win = nil
                end
            end
        end
        M.open_terminal(cur_n)
    
        local label = layout == "dock" and "Docked Split (Bottom)"
            or (layout == "bottom_float" and "Bottom Floating Overlay" or "Centered Floating Popup")
        vim.notify("🖥️ Terminal layout set to: " .. label, vim.log.levels.INFO, { title = "Multi-Terminal" })
    end
    
    --- Toggles terminal layout cycling through: dock -> bottom_float -> float -> dock.
    function M.toggle_layout()
        local cur = get_terminal_layout()
        local target
        if cur == "dock" then
            target = "bottom_float"
        elseif cur == "bottom_float" then
            target = "float"
        else
            target = "dock"
        end
        M.set_layout(target)
    end
    
    M.is_term_buf = is_term_buf
    M.is_term_win = is_term_win
    
    --- Resizes the terminal height.
    --- In bottom floating mode: stretches or shrinks from the top edge, keeping bottom edge anchored.
    --- In centered floating mode: stretches or shrinks top and bottom edges simultaneously (centered).
    --- In docked split mode: adjusts the split window height upwards or downwards.
    --- Persists the new height globally across sessions in stdpath("state").
    --- @param delta integer Positive to grow, negative to shrink.
    --- @return boolean resized Whether resizing took place.
    function M.resize_height(delta)
        delta = delta or M.settings.resize_step or 2
        local win = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
        local active_win = get_active_terminal_win()
        local target_win = nil
    
        if is_valid_win(win) and is_term_win(win) then
            target_win = win
        elseif is_valid_win(active_win) then
            target_win = active_win
        end
    
        if not target_win or not is_valid_win(target_win) then
            return false
        end
    
        local total_lines = vim.o.lines or 24
        local is_float = is_floating_win(target_win)
    
        if is_float then
            local cfg = vim.api.nvim_win_get_config(target_win)
            local cur_h = cfg.height
            local cur_r = type(cfg.row) == "table" and (cfg.row[false] or 1) or (tonumber(cfg.row) or 1)
    
            local layout = get_terminal_layout()
            local is_bottom = (layout == "bottom_float") or (cur_r + cur_h >= total_lines - 4)
    
            if is_bottom then
                -- Bottom floating: bottom edge is anchored; stretch or shrink from top edge
                local bottom_edge = cur_r + cur_h
                local max_h = math.max(M.settings.min_height, bottom_edge - 1)
                local new_h = math.min(max_h, math.max(M.settings.min_height, cur_h + delta))
                local new_r = bottom_edge - new_h
    
                if new_h ~= cur_h or new_r ~= cur_r then
                    local ok = pcall(vim.api.nvim_win_set_config, target_win, {
                        relative = cfg.relative or "editor",
                        row = new_r,
                        col = cfg.col,
                        width = cfg.width,
                        height = new_h,
                    })
                    if ok then
                        save_bottom_float_height(new_h)
                        return true
                    end
                end
            else
                -- Centered floating: stretches or shrinks top and bottom edge at the same time (stays centered)
                local max_h = math.max(M.settings.min_height, total_lines - 4)
                local new_h = math.min(max_h, math.max(M.settings.min_height, cur_h + delta))
                local new_r = math.max(1, math.floor((total_lines - new_h) / 2) - 1)
    
                if new_h ~= cur_h or new_r ~= cur_r then
                    local ok = pcall(vim.api.nvim_win_set_config, target_win, {
                        relative = cfg.relative or "editor",
                        row = new_r,
                        col = cfg.col,
                        width = cfg.width,
                        height = new_h,
                    })
                    if ok then
                        save_float_height(new_h)
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
            return false
        end
    
        -- Docked bottom split: increasing height stretches from top edge upwards!
        local cur_h = vim.api.nvim_win_get_height(target_win)
        local max_h = math.max(M.settings.min_height, total_lines - 4)
        local new_h = math.min(max_h, math.max(M.settings.min_height, cur_h + delta))
    
        if new_h ~= cur_h then
            pcall(vim.api.nvim_win_set_height, target_win, new_h)
            save_height(new_h)
            return true
        end
    
        return false
    end
    
    --- Stretches the terminal height larger (<C-Up>).
    function M.increase_height()
        local step = M.settings.resize_step or 2
        M.resize_height(step)
    end
    
    --- Shrinks the terminal height smaller (<C-Down>).
    function M.decrease_height()
        local step = M.settings.resize_step or 2
        M.resize_height(-step)
    end
    
    --- Re-exported for callers that relied on this module owning the dock order.
    M.enforce_bottom_layout = dock.enforce_order
    
    -- ============================================================================
    -- SETUP
    -- ============================================================================
    
    --- Pastes the OS clipboard into a terminal (`"+`, falling back to `"*`).
    local function paste_clipboard()
        local clip = vim.fn.getreg("+")
        if not clip or clip == "" then
            clip = vim.fn.getreg("*")
        end
        if clip and clip ~= "" then
            vim.api.nvim_paste(clip, true, -1)
        end
    end
    
    --- Binds selection, toggling, closing and clipboard keys, and starts the
    --- autocmd that remembers a resized terminal's height.
    function M.setup()
        if M._did_setup then
            return
        end
        M._did_setup = true
    
        sync_terminals()
    
        vim.api.nvim_create_autocmd("WinResized", {
            group = vim.api.nvim_create_augroup("FoxTerminalHeightSaver", { clear = true }),
            callback = function()
                for _, winid in ipairs(vim.api.nvim_list_wins()) do
                    if vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(winid) then
                        local bufnr = vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(winid)
                        if vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(bufnr) and vim.bo[bufnr].buftype == "terminal" then
                            save_height(vim.api.nvim_win_get_height(winid))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end,
        })
    
        for n = 1, M.settings.count do
            for _, prefix in ipairs({ M.settings.keys.select_prefix, "<M-" }) do
                vim.keymap.set({ "n", "t" }, prefix .. n .. ">", function()
                    M.select_terminal(n)
                end, { noremap = true, silent = true, desc = "Select Terminal #" .. n })
                vim.keymap.set("i", prefix .. n .. ">", "<C-\\><C-n><Cmd>TerminalSelect " .. n .. "<CR>", {
                    silent = true,
                    desc = "Select Terminal #" .. n,
                })
            end
        end
    
        vim.api.nvim_create_user_command("TerminalToggle", function()
            M.toggle_selected_terminal()
        end, { desc = "Toggle selected terminal window" })
    
        vim.api.nvim_create_user_command("TerminalKillCurrent", function()
            M.kill_current_terminal()
        end, { desc = "Kill only the currently open terminal" })
    
        vim.api.nvim_create_user_command("TerminalToggleLayout", function()
            M.toggle_layout()
        end, { desc = "Toggle terminal layout between Docked Split and Floating Overlay" })
    
        vim.api.nvim_create_user_command("TerminalLayout", function(opts)
            if opts.args and opts.args ~= "" then
                M.set_layout(opts.args)
            else
                M.toggle_layout()
            end
        end, {
            nargs = "?",
            complete = function()
                return { "dock", "float", "bottom_float" }
            end,
            desc = "Set or toggle terminal layout (dock, float, bottom_float)",
        })
    
        vim.api.nvim_create_user_command("TerminalIncreaseHeight", function()
            M.increase_height()
        end, { desc = "Increase terminal height (<C-Up>)" })
    
        vim.api.nvim_create_user_command("TerminalDecreaseHeight", function()
            M.decrease_height()
        end, { desc = "Decrease terminal height (<C-Down>)" })
    
        vim.api.nvim_create_user_command("TerminalSelect", function(opts)
            local num = tonumber(opts.args)
            if num then
                M.select_terminal(num)
            else
                M.toggle_selected_terminal()
            end
        end, { nargs = "?", desc = "Select terminal by number" })
    
        local toggle_keys = vim.list_extend(vim.deepcopy(M.settings.keys.toggle), { "<M-;>" })
        for _, key in ipairs(toggle_keys) do
            vim.keymap.set({ "n", "t" }, key, M.toggle_selected_terminal, {
                noremap = true,
                silent = true,
                desc = "Toggle Selected Terminal",
            })
            -- Leave insert before BufLeave's stopinsert can consume the first terminal character.
            vim.keymap.set("i", key, "<C-\\><C-n><Cmd>TerminalToggle<CR>", {
                silent = true,
                desc = "Toggle Selected Terminal",
            })
        end
    
        -- Note: <C-w> in terminal mode is preserved for native shell word deletion (Ctrl+W)
        if M.settings.keys.close and M.settings.keys.close ~= "<C-w>" then
            vim.keymap.set("t", M.settings.keys.close, function()
                pcall(vim.cmd, "stopinsert")
                if _G.Neotree_Smart_Quit then
                    _G.Neotree_Smart_Quit()
                else
                    pcall(vim.cmd, "close")
                end
            end, { noremap = true, silent = true, nowait = true, desc = "Close Terminal Window" })
        end
    
        for _, key in ipairs(M.settings.keys.paste) do
            vim.keymap.set("t", key, paste_clipboard, {
                noremap = true,
                silent = true,
                desc = "Paste OS Clipboard to Terminal",
            })
        end
        for _, key in ipairs(M.settings.keys.copy) do
            vim.keymap.set("v", key, '"+y', {
                noremap = true,
                silent = true,
                desc = "Copy selection to OS Clipboard",
            })
        end
    
        -- Auto-enter terminal mode on buffer focus.
        -- Ensures returning to a terminal window (e.g. toggling Neo-tree off)
        -- automatically puts Neovim into terminal (insert) mode (`startinsert`).
        local auto_insert_group = vim.api.nvim_create_augroup("FoxTerminalAutoInsert", { clear = true })
    
    local t = require("fox.lib.foxnvim.test")
    local terminal = require("plugins.fox.dev.terminal")
    
    t.describe("terminal return focus", function()
        t.it("exchanges Git Center and terminal panes while preserving unrelated floats and terminal contents", function()
            local cwd = vim.fn.getcwd()
            vim.api.nvim_set_current_dir(vim.fn.stdpath("config"))
            local center = require("plugins.fox.git.git_center")
            local config = require("plugins.fox.git.git_center.config")
            local original, selected = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win(), terminal.get_selected_terminal()
            center.close_git_center()
            config.cached_view, config.cached_view_data = nil, nil
            local buf = vim.api.nvim_create_buf(false, true)
            vim.b[buf].fox_is_multi_term, vim.b[buf].fox_term_num = true, 9
            vim.b[buf].fox_env_slot = _G._fox_active_env_slot or 1
            vim.api.nvim_buf_set_lines(buf, 0, -1, false, { "terminal output survives" })
            local pane = vim.api.nvim_open_win(buf, true, { split = "below", height = 3 })
            local other_buf, other = require("fox.core.ui").float({ lines = { "unrelated panel" }, width = 20, height = 2 })
            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                center.open_git_center()
                t.expect(center.is_open()).toBe(true)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(pane)).toBe(false)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(other)).toBe(true)
                terminal.open_terminal(9)
                t.expect(center.is_open()).toBe(false)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf()).toBe(buf)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_buf_get_lines(buf, 0, -1, false)[1]).toBe("terminal output survives")
                center.open_git_center()
                terminal.select_terminal(9)
                t.expect(center.is_open()).toBe(false)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf()).toBe(buf)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(other)).toBe(true)
            end)
            pcall(vim.cmd, "stopinsert")
            center.close_git_center()
            terminal.hide_terminal()
            if vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(other) then
                vim.api.nvim_win_close(other, true)
            end
            if vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(other_buf) then
                vim.api.nvim_buf_delete(other_buf, { force = true })
            end
            vim.api.nvim_buf_delete(buf, { force = true })
            _G._fox_selected_terminal = selected
            if vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(original) then
                vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(original)
            end
            vim.api.nvim_set_current_dir(cwd)
            assert(ok, err)
        end)
        t.it("returns to a floating panel after focusing an already visible terminal", function()
            local original = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
            local buf = vim.api.nvim_create_buf(false, true)
            vim.b[buf].fox_is_multi_term = true
            vim.b[buf].fox_term_num = 9
            vim.b[buf].fox_env_slot = _G._fox_active_env_slot or 1
            local win = vim.api.nvim_open_win(buf, false, { split = "below", height = 3 })
            local panel_buf = vim.api.nvim_create_buf(false, true)
            local panel = vim.api.nvim_open_win(panel_buf, false, {
                relative = "editor",
                row = 1,
                col = 1,
                width = 30,
                height = 5,
            })
            local selected = terminal.get_selected_terminal()
            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                terminal.open_terminal(9)
                vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(panel)
                terminal.toggle_selected_terminal()
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()).toBe(win)
                terminal.toggle_selected_terminal()
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()).toBe(panel)
            end)
            pcall(vim.cmd, "stopinsert")
            for _, w in ipairs({ panel, win }) do
                if vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(w) then
                    vim.api.nvim_win_close(w, true)
                end
            end
            vim.api.nvim_buf_delete(buf, { force = true })
            vim.api.nvim_buf_delete(panel_buf, { force = true })
            _G._fox_selected_terminal = selected
            vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(original)
            assert(ok, err)
        end)
        t.it("kills only the visible terminal job and leaves hidden slots running", function()
            local original = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
            local original_buf = vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf()
            local selected = terminal.get_selected_terminal()
            local buffers, jobs = {}, {}
            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                for i, slot in ipairs({ 8, 9 }) do
                    local buf = vim.api.nvim_create_buf(false, true)
                    buffers[i] = buf
                    vim.api.nvim_set_current_buf(buf)
                    vim.b[buf].fox_is_multi_term = true
                    vim.b[buf].fox_term_num = slot
                    vim.b[buf].fox_env_slot = _G._fox_active_env_slot or 1
                    jobs[i] = vim.fn.jobstart({ vim.v.progpath, "--headless", "-u", "NONE", "-c", "sleep 60" }, { term = true })
                    assert(jobs[i] > 0, "terminal job failed to start")
                end
                vim.api.nvim_set_current_buf(original_buf)
                terminal.open_terminal(9, false)
                local pane = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
                -- Palette actions can execute with focus in an editor window.
                vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(original)
                terminal.kill_current_terminal()
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(buffers[2])).toBe(false)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(pane)).toBe(false)
                t.expect(vim.wait(2000, function()
                    return vim.fn.jobwait({ jobs[2] }, 0)[1] ~= -1
                end)).toBe(true)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(buffers[1])).toBe(true)
                t.expect(vim.fn.jobwait({ jobs[1] }, 0)[1]).toBe(-1)
                terminal.kill_current_terminal()
                t.expect(vim.fn.jobwait({ jobs[1] }, 0)[1]).toBe(-1)
            end)
            for _, job in ipairs(jobs) do
                pcall(vim.fn.jobstop, job)
            end
            for _, buf in ipairs(buffers) do
                if vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(buf) then
                    vim.api.nvim_buf_delete(buf, { force = true })
                end
            end
            _G._fox_selected_terminal = selected
            vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(original)
            vim.api.nvim_set_current_buf(original_buf)
            assert(ok, err)
        end)
    end)

    --- Extracts a clean, concise process title from vim.b.term_title or raw terminal URL.
    --- @param str string Raw buffer name or term:// string
    --- @return string|nil title
    function M.get_term_title(str)
        local title = vim.b and vim.b.term_title
        if type(title) == "string" and title ~= "" and not title:find("^term://") then
            title = title:gsub("^Administrator:%s*", "")
            title = title:gsub("^Windows PowerShell", "powershell")
            if title:find("[/\\]") then
                title = vim.fn.fnamemodify(title, ":t")
            end
            title = title:gsub("%.[eE][xX][eE]$", "")
            title = vim.trim(title)
            if #title > 30 then
                title = title:sub(1, 27) .. "..."
            end
            if title ~= "" then
                return title
            end
        end
    
        if str then
            local shell_path = str:match("//%d+:(.*)$") or str:match(".*:(.*)$")
            if shell_path and shell_path ~= "" then
                shell_path = shell_path:gsub("\\", "/")
                local shell_name = vim.fn.fnamemodify(shell_path, ":t"):gsub("%.[eE][xX][eE]$", "")
                if shell_name ~= "" then
                    return shell_name
                end
            end
        end
    
        return nil
    end
    
    --- Formats filename for statusline, simplifying raw terminal URLs (term://...) into clean labels.
    --- @param str string
    --- @return string formatted
    function M.format_filename(str)
        if not str or str == "" then
            return "[No Name]"
        end

    function M.process_agent(name, command)
        name = basename(name)
        if names[name] then
            return name
        end
        if
            not ({ node = true, bun = true, deno = true, python = true, python3 = true, npx = true, uv = true, uvx = true })[name]
        then
            return nil
        end
        -- Inspect script paths too: npm's agent processes often have node.exe as their name.
        command = type(command) == "string" and command or ""
        command = command:gsub("([\"'])(.-)%1", function(_, value)
            return value:gsub("%s", "\1")
        end)
        local first = true
        for token in command:gmatch("%S+") do
            token = token:gsub("\1", " ")
            local candidate = basename(token)
            if first then
                first = false
            elseif token:sub(1, 1) ~= "-" then
                local path = token:lower():gsub("\\", "/")
                if path:find("@anthropic-ai/claude-code/", 1, true) then
                    return "claude"
                end
                if path:find("@google/gemini-cli/", 1, true) then
                    return "gemini"
                end
                for part in path:gmatch("[^/]+") do
                    if names[part] then
                        return part
                    end
                end
                return names[candidate] and candidate or nil
            end
        end
    end
    
    function M.find_agent(processes, root)
        local children = {}
        for _, process in ipairs(processes) do
            if process.pid == root then
                local agent = M.process_agent(process.name, process.command)
                if agent then
                    return agent
                end
            end
            children[process.ppid] = children[process.ppid] or {}
            table.insert(children[process.ppid], process)
        end
        local seen = {}
        local function visit(pid)
            if seen[pid] then
                return nil
            end
            seen[pid] = true

        local function setup_term_buffer(bufnr)
            if is_term_buf(bufnr) then
                vim.keymap.set("t", "<C-Space>", "<C-\\><C-n>", {
                    buffer = bufnr,
                    silent = true,
                    desc = "Navigate terminal output",
                })
                for _, key in ipairs({ "<C-u>", "<C-d>" }) do
                    vim.keymap.set("t", key, "<C-\\><C-n>" .. key, {
                        buffer = bufnr,
                        silent = true,
                        desc = "Scroll terminal output",
                    })
                end
                vim.keymap.set("x", "y", '"+y', {
                    buffer = bufnr,
                    silent = true,
                    desc = "Copy terminal selection to clipboard",
                })
                for _, k in ipairs(M.settings.keys.resize_up or {}) do
                    pcall(vim.keymap.set, { "n", "t", "i" }, k, function()
                        M.increase_height()
                    end, {
                        buffer = bufnr,
                        noremap = true,
                        silent = true,
                        desc = "Terminal Stretch Bigger (Increase Height)",
                    })
                end
                for _, k in ipairs(M.settings.keys.resize_down or {}) do
                    pcall(vim.keymap.set, { "n", "t", "i" }, k, function()
                        M.decrease_height()
                    end, {
                        buffer = bufnr,
                        noremap = true,
                        silent = true,
                        desc = "Terminal Shrink Smaller (Decrease Height)",
                    })
                end
            end
        end
    
        local function enter_terminal_mode(bufnr)
            if not is_term_buf(bufnr) then
                return
            end
            if vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf() == bufnr and vim.api.nvim_get_mode().mode ~= "t" then
                pcall(vim.cmd, "startinsert")
            end
        end
    
        for _, bufnr in ipairs(vim.api.nvim_list_bufs()) do
            if is_term_buf(bufnr) then
                setup_term_buffer(bufnr)
            end
        end
    
        vim.api.nvim_create_autocmd({ "TermOpen", "BufEnter", "WinEnter", "BufWinEnter" }, {
            group = auto_insert_group,
            callback = function(args)
                if is_term_buf(args.buf) then
                    setup_term_buffer(args.buf)
                    enter_terminal_mode(args.buf)
                    vim.schedule(function()
                        if vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf() == args.buf then
                            enter_terminal_mode(args.buf)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end,
        })
    end

    -- ============================================================================
    -- tests/integration/run.lua -- Entry point for tests that need the REAL editor.
    -- ============================================================================
    -- WHY A SECOND RUNNER
    --   tests/run.lua runs under `nvim -l` with no plugins, so it only covers pure
    --   logic. Anything that needs nvim-dap, telescope or the actual startup sequence
    --   has to boot the full config -- that is what this runner does.
    --
    -- HOW TO RUN
    --   nvim --headless -S tests/integration/run.lua -- --only-failure
    --   nvim --headless -S tests/integration/run.lua -- dap --only-failure    # name filter
    --
    -- WRITING AN INTEGRATION SPEC
    --   Same shape as tests/spec (foxnvim.test describe/it/expect), but plugins may
    --   be assumed loaded. Load what you need explicitly at the top of the spec:
    --     require("lazy").load({ plugins = { "nvim-dap" } })
    -- ============================================================================
    
    local root = vim.fn.fnamemodify(debug.getinfo(1, "S").source:sub(2), ":p:h:h:h")
    local arguments = {}
    local found_script = false
    local function script_path(value)
        local path = vim.fs.normalize(vim.fn.fnamemodify(value, ":p"))
        return vim.fn.has("win32") == 1 and path:lower() or path
    end
    for _, argument in ipairs(vim.v.argv or {}) do
        if found_script then
            arguments[#arguments + 1] = argument
        elseif script_path(argument) == script_path(debug.getinfo(1, "S").source:sub(2)) then
            found_script = true
        end
    end
    local function run()
        local filter, options = require("fox.lib.foxnvim.test").parse_args(arguments)
    
        local files = {}
        for _, file in ipairs(vim.fn.glob(root .. "/tests/integration/*_spec.lua", false, true)) do
            local name = vim.fn.fnamemodify(file, ":t:r")
            if not filter or name:find(filter, 1, true) then
                files[#files + 1] = file
            end
        end
    
        local child_spec = vim.env.FOX_TEST_INTEGRATION_SPEC
        if not child_spec then
            -- Each spec owns editor-wide fixtures: cwd, buffers, mappings and lazy loading.
            local passed, failed, os_dependent = 0, 0, 0
            for _, file in ipairs(files) do
                local command = { vim.v.progpath, "--headless", "-i", "NONE", "-S", root .. "/tests/integration/run.lua", "--" }
                if options.only_failure then
                    command[#command + 1] = "--only-failure"
                end
                local environment = vim.fn.environ()
                -- Supplying env opts otherwise reintroduces the outer editor's RPC socket.
                environment.NVIM, environment.NVIM_LISTEN_ADDRESS = nil, nil
                environment.FOX_TEST_INTEGRATION_SPEC = file
                local stdout_file, stderr_file
                if vim.fn.has("win32") == 1 then
                    -- Use real files so descendants cannot keep the runner's capture pipes open.
                    stdout_file, stderr_file = vim.fn.tempname(), vim.fn.tempname()
                    local python = vim.fn.exepath("python")
                    if python == "" then
                        python = vim.fn.exepath("python3")
                    end
                    assert(python ~= "", "Windows integration tests require Python for isolated process IO")
                    command = {
                        python,
                        "-c",
                        [[
    import subprocess, sys
    command = [sys.argv[1], '--headless', '-i', 'NONE', '-S', sys.argv[2], '--']
    if sys.argv[5] == '1':
        command.append('--only-failure')
    with open(sys.argv[3], 'wb') as stdout, open(sys.argv[4], 'wb') as stderr:
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(command, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr,
                                    close_fds=True, creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW, timeout=120)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            sys.exit(124)
    sys.exit(result.returncode)

    --- @param buf integer|nil

    local function is_valid_buf(buf)
        return type(buf) == "number" and vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(buf)
    end
    
    --- True when bufnr belongs to a terminal.
    --- @param bufnr integer|nil
    --- @return boolean
    local function is_term_buf(bufnr)
        return is_valid_buf(bufnr) and (vim.bo[bufnr].buftype == "terminal" or vim.b[bufnr].fox_is_multi_term)
    end
    
    --- True when win displays a terminal buffer.
    --- @param win integer|nil
    --- @return boolean
    local function is_term_win(win)
        if not is_valid_win(win) then
            return false
        end
        local buf = vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(win)
        return is_term_buf(buf)
    end

## Assistant

El título ya se comprobó con una secuencia real enviada por una terminal, y mover a un slot ocupado conserva ambos buffers. La recuperación exacta sigue siendo la parte delicada: las herramientas pueden abrir varias conversaciones simultáneas y «reanudar la última» mezclaría chats.

            if t.kind != "symbol" {
                continue
            }
            switch t.text {
            case "(":
                depth++
            case ")":
                depth--
                if depth < 0 {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unbalanced SQL parentheses")
                }
            case ";":
                if depth != 0 {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unsupported SQL statement body")
                }
                pieces = append(pieces, s[first:t.start])
                first = t.end
            }
        }
        if depth != 0 {
            return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unbalanced SQL parentheses")
        }
        pieces = append(pieces, s[first:])
        out := []statement{}
        for _, piece := range pieces {
            ts, _ := lex(piece, engine)
            if len(ts) == 0 {
                continue
            }
            st := statement{sql: piece, autoSafe: ts[0].kind == "word" && strings.EqualFold(ts[0].text, "SELECT")}
            // A nested WHERE does not limit the outer UPDATE or DELETE.
            level, mutation, where := 0, false, false
            for _, t := range ts {
                if t.kind == "symbol" {
                    if t.text == "(" {
                        level++
                    }
                    if t.text == ")" {
                        level--
                    }
                }
                if t.kind != "word" {
                    continue
                }
                w := strings.ToUpper(t.text)
                if strings.Contains(" INSERT UPDATE DELETE MERGE CREATE ALTER DROP TRUNCATE ATTACH DETACH PRAGMA COPY CALL DO SET RESET INTO LOCK UNLOCK GRANT REVOKE VACUUM ANALYZE ANALYSE LOAD ", " "+w+" ") {
                    st.autoSafe = false
                }
                if strings.Contains(" DROP TRUNCATE ", " "+w+" ") {
                    st.confirmation = true
                }
                if w == "UPDATE" || w == "DELETE" {
                    if level != 0 || mutation {
                        st.confirmation = true
                    }
                    mutation = true
                    where = false
                }
                if mutation && w == "WHERE" && level == 0 {
                    where = true
                }
            }
            if mutation && !where {
                st.confirmation = true
            }
            for i, t := range ts {
                if t.kind != "symbol" || t.text != "?" {
                    continue
                }
                if engine == "postgres" {
                    if i+1 < len(ts) && (ts[i+1].text == "|" || ts[i+1].text == "&") {
                        continue
                    }
                    if i > 0 {
                        p := ts[i-1]
                        if p.kind == "identifier" || p.kind == "string" || p.text == ")" || p.text == "]" || p.kind == "word" && !strings.Contains(" SELECT WHERE AND OR WHEN THEN ELSE LIMIT OFFSET BY AS DISTINCT BETWEEN LIKE ILIKE IS IN ", " "+strings.ToUpper(p.text)+" ") {
                            continue
                        }
                    }
                }
                if i+1 < len(ts) && ts[i+1].start == t.end && ts[i+1].text[0] >= '0' && ts[i+1].text[0] <= '9' {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Use plain ? placeholders, not numbered placeholders")
                }
                st.positions = append(st.positions, t.start)
            }
            out = append(out, st)

    local t = require("fox.lib.foxnvim.test")
    
    t.describe("WakaTime CLI installation", function()
        t.it("installs asynchronously, prevents duplicate installs, and allows retry after failure", function()
            local tracker = require("fox.wakatime")
            local installer = dofile(vim.fn.stdpath("config") .. "/lua/fox/wakatime/install.lua")
            local saved_cli, saved_system, saved_notify = tracker.cli, vim.system, vim.notify
            local saved_executable, saved_exepath = vim.fn.executable, vim.fn.exepath
            vim.fn.executable = function()
                return 1
            end
            vim.fn.exepath = function()
                return "fake-python"
            end
            local installed, callback, notifications = false, nil, {}
            local calls = 0
            tracker.cli = function()
                return installed and "installed-cli" or nil
            end
            vim.notify = function(message)
                table.insert(notifications, message)
            end
            vim.system = function(args, opts, done)
                calls = calls + 1
                t.expect(args[2]:find("install_wakatime.py", 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
                t.expect(args[3]:find(".wakatime/wakatime-cli", 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
                t.expect(opts.detach).toBe(nil)
                callback = done
            end
            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                installer.run()
                installer.run()
                t.expect(calls).toBe(1)
                callback({ code = 1, stderr = "download failed" })
                t.expect(vim.wait(1000, function()
                    return (notifications[#notifications] or ""):find("download failed", 1, true) ~= nil
                end, 10)).toBe(true)
                installer.run()
                t.expect(calls).toBe(2)
                installed = true
                callback({ code = 0 })
                t.expect(vim.wait(1000, function()
                    return (notifications[#notifications] or ""):find("Set your tracking key", 1, true) ~= nil
                end, 10)).toBe(true)
                installer.run()
                t.expect(calls).toBe(2)
                installed = false
                vim.fn.executable = function()
                    return 0
                end
                installer.run()
                t.expect(calls).toBe(2)
                t.expect(notifications[#notifications]:find("Install Python 3", 1, true) ~= nil).toBe(true)
            end)
            tracker.cli, vim.system, vim.notify = saved_cli, saved_system, saved_notify
            vim.fn.executable, vim.fn.exepath = saved_executable, saved_exepath
            assert(ok, err)
        end)
    end)

    $ cat lua/fox/wakatime/install.lua; nvim -l tests/run.lua wakatime_install --only-failure
    local M = {}
    local installing = false
    
    function M.run()
        if installing then
            vim.notify("WakaTime CLI installation is already running.")
            return
        end
        local existing = require("fox.wakatime").cli()
        if existing then
            vim.notify("WakaTime CLI is already installed: " .. existing)
            return
        end
        local windows = vim.fn.has("win32") == 1
        local python
        for _, name in ipairs(windows and { "python", "python3" } or { "python3", "python" }) do
            if vim.fn.executable(name) == 1 then
                python = vim.fn.exepath(name)
                break
            end
        end
        if not python then
            vim.notify("Install Python 3, then run :WakaTimeInstall again.", vim.log.levels.ERROR)
            return
        end
        local destination = (vim.env.WAKATIME_HOME or vim.fn.expand("~"))
            .. "/.wakatime/wakatime-cli"
            .. (windows and ".exe" or "")
        installing = true
        vim.notify("Downloading and verifying the official WakaTime CLI...")
        local ok, err = pcall(
            vim.system,
            {
                python,
                vim.fn.stdpath("config") .. "/config/scripts/dependencies/install_wakatime.py",
                destination,
            },
            { text = true, timeout = 180000 },
            vim.schedule_wrap(function(result)
                installing = false
                if result.code == 0 and require("fox.wakatime").cli() then
                    vim.notify("WakaTime CLI installed. Set your tracking key with :WakaTimeApiKey.")
                else
                    vim.notify("WakaTime CLI installation failed: " .. vim.trim(result.stderr or ""), vim.log.levels.ERROR)
                end
            end)
        )
        if not ok then
            installing = false
            vim.notify("Could not start WakaTime CLI installation: " .. tostring(err), vim.log.levels.ERROR)
        end
    end
    
    return M

## Assistant

Implementé parte de lo solicitado:

- Query Mode permite ejecutar escrituras. `UPDATE` y `DELETE` sin `WHERE`, además de `DROP` y `TRUNCATE`, piden escribir una palabra aleatoria antes de ejecutarse. La ejecución automática sigue limitada a consultas de lectura.
- Dentro de una terminal, **Ctrl+Shift+Alt+1…9** mueve la terminal al slot elegido o la intercambia si está ocupado.
- Las ventanas flotantes muestran el nombre de la terminal y lo actualizan cuando cambia.

**Quedan pendientes dos partes:** la restauración de los chats exactos de Codex, Claude, OpenCode y agy después de reiniciar Neovim —incluida su reordenación— y el bug al cambiar de environment. La prueba de volver al editor con una pestaña SQL abierta pasa, pero necesito saber qué comportamiento incorrecto ves para reproducirlo.

Las pruebas generales terminaron con **1193 aprobadas**; integración tuvo **148 aprobadas y 2 fallos** ajenos a estos cambios, en Neo-tree y el parser de Protobuf. Una prueba dependiente de Windows quedó sin ejecutar.

El analizador SQL aún no admite cuerpos compuestos de rutinas o triggers con sentencias internas. Tampoco comprobé las escrituras contra servidores MySQL o PostgreSQL reales; Windows se compiló, pero no se ejecutó.
