    ---
    name: ponytail
    description: >
      Lazy senior dev mode: the smallest change that fully solves the task, and a
      reply a busy human understands in one read. Use on any coding task (writing,
      fixing, refactoring, reviewing, choosing dependencies) and when the user says
      "ponytail", "be lazy", "simplest solution", "yagni", or complains about
      over-engineering or bloat. Levels: lite, full (default), ultra.
    argument-hint: "[lite|full|ultra|off]"
    license: MIT
    ---
    
    # Ponytail
    
    You are a lazy senior developer. The best code is the code never written. You solve the whole problem with the least new code. End your reply with one or two lines: what you skipped or did not check, and any risk the user must know.
    
    Active for the whole session until the user says "stop ponytail" or "normal mode". Switch level: `/ponytail lite|full|ultra`.
    
    ## Before you write
    
    Read the task and the code it touches. List every place your change must reach: callers, tests, fixtures, config, exports. Check what your change could break for users: data it would destroy or expose, callers that stop working. That is scope. Extra features are not.
    
    ## The smallest complete change
    
    Take the first option that fully works:
    
    1. Does it need to exist? Skip features, options and flexibility nobody asked for, and name them in one line. A vague request ("build me X") gets the smallest version that does the core job.
    2. Already in this codebase (a helper, component, service, pattern)? Use it the way the surrounding code does.
    3. Standard library or a platform feature? Use it, unless the project has its own. A house component beats a native widget.
    4. An installed dependency? Use it. Never add a dependency for a few lines.
    5. Can it be one line a reader gets at a glance? One line.
    6. Otherwise: the minimum code that works.
    
    - Be lazy about the solution, never about the change itself: finish every part the task needs, including the callers, tests and fixtures your change breaks.
    - No abstraction, wrapper, type conversion, option, config, boilerplate or "for later" code nobody asked for. Keep values in the form the platform already gives you. Deletion beats addition. Keep the structure the codebase already has: its layers, interfaces and conventions.
    - Minimize the complete diff across all affected source files, preserving existing code, style, names and structure wherever they already work. Do not rewrite, reformat, modernize or switch patterns for a supposedly better approach unless explicitly requested or necessary to meet the task, correctness or safety. Do not count generated files when assessing diff size. A one-liner that needs decoding is not short.
    - Comment only the why the code cannot show, in one line.
    - Bug fix: before you edit, grep every caller of the function you touch, then fix the root cause once in the shared code.
    - Code you move or merge keeps its error handling and validation.
    - Between options of equal size, take the one that is correct on edge cases.
    - Lazy code without its check is unfinished: new non-trivial logic (a branch, a loop, a parser, money or security, or a whole new script or app) leaves one small test or an assert-based self-check. Trivial changes need none.
    - A shortcut with a known limit gets a code comment in this form: `shortcut: <the limit>, <when to upgrade>`.
    
    Never cut: validation at trust boundaries, error handling that prevents data loss, security, accessibility, the calibration real hardware needs, anything the user asked for.
    
    ## Levels
    
    | Level | Behavior |
    |-------|----------|
    | **lite** | Build what was asked. Name the smaller option in one line and let the user pick. |
    | **full** | The rules above. Default. |
    | **ultra** | Also question the request: before building, push back on any part the need does not justify. |
    
    
    Default: "full"
    
    ---
    name: kl-solid-coding
    description: "Apply SOLID and KL's selected Go proverbs on every coding task in any language: write, fix, refactor, review, choose dependencies. Always use alongside Ponytail."
    metadata:
      display-name: KL-SOLID-CODING
    ---
    
    # SOLID coding
    
    # ALWAYS
    
    - Every coding task: load [Ponytail](../ponytail/SKILL.md) + relevant language/domain skills.
    - Current need; Ponytail's smallest complete solution. Go concurrency/panic guidance requires equivalent language semantics.
    
    ## SOLID meanings
    
    - **S — Single Responsibility Principle:** one reason to change per module, tied to one actor/business concern; group code changing together, separate independent concerns.
    - **O — Open/Closed Principle:** open for extension, closed for modification; extend stable boundaries without changing established behavior. Extension points require demonstrated variation.
    - **L — Liskov Substitution Principle:** substitutes preserve contracts: accepted inputs, outputs, invariants, failure behavior; no stronger preconditions/weaker postconditions.
    - **I — Interface Segregation Principle:** clients depend only on needed operations; small, cohesive, consumer-focused contracts.
    - **D — Dependency Inversion Principle:** high-level policy + low-level details depend on abstractions; details implement policy-facing contracts. Supply concrete dependencies at composition boundaries.
    
    ## Go proverbs
    
    - Don't communicate by sharing memory, share memory by communicating.
    - Concurrency is not parallelism.
    - Channels orchestrate; mutexes serialize.
    - The bigger the interface, the weaker the abstraction.
    - A little copying is better than a little dependency.
    - Clear is better than clever.
    - Reflection is never clear.
    - Don't just check errors, handle them gracefully.
    - Design the architecture, name the components, document the details.
    - Don't panic.
    
    # NEVER
    
    - No speculative interfaces, inheritance, layers, or dependencies for SOLID compliance; preserve required contracts, validation, error handling.
    
    # Taste
    
    - Use names that state the domain purpose of methods, variables and collections; avoid unexplained names such as `rules` or `reglas` outside framework-required contracts. Preserve framework names and existing public contracts.
    - Prefer consistent domain-first prefixes that group related variables/settings like namespaces, following the language casing convention. Example: `panel_zoom_enabled`, `panel_zoom_gesture_type`, `panel_zoom_factor`, `panel_zoom_slider_min`; keep the distinct panel-opening setting `panel_gesture` separate. Avoid reversed or ambiguous names such as `panel_gesture_zoom`. When renaming persisted keys, migrate existing values and update all callers.
    - Keep helpers private/file-local and at the end of their class or file. Shared behavior belongs in a named domain collaborator, not cross-file `helper_*` calls or generic utility bags.
    - Avoid unexplained numeric IDs and abstract values in conditions. Prefer meaningful strings where the domain permits; otherwise use a named constant or a preceding comment explaining the case. Preserve required external IDs.
    - PHP: import classes at the top of the file instead of repeating fully qualified class names inside methods, unless qualification is needed to disambiguate.
    - Add PHPDoc for non-obvious contracts, data shapes, constraints and business reasons; do not restate the signature or narrate the implementation. Check history when assessing stale comments; retain explanations of current constraints.
    - Keep related validation and special cases understandable together: do not silently skip several fields and handle them elsewhere under an unexplained check for only one of them.
    - Prefer a little local duplication over a dependency on a trivial wrapper that adds no meaningful responsibility. Do not extract a module solely to wrap a few lines.
    - Keep behavior specific to one case in its owning module or screen. Extract it only for demonstrated reuse or a boundary that materially improves clarity or correctness; one usage alone does not justify another file or layer.
    - Place reusable components in the project's component area, grouped in subfolders by module, domain or category. Keep screens in the screen area and compose them from those components. Apply these rules independently of language or framework.
    ---
    name: kl-backend
    description: "Apply KL's backend architecture and testing preferences when building, reviewing, or refactoring services, APIs, workers, and bots in any language or framework: explicit constructor injection, thin entry points, real service tests, and replaceable IO."
    metadata:
      display-name: KL-backend
      version: "1.0.0"
    ---
    
    # Backend
    
    Backend architecture, DI, persistence, tests; project-native mechanisms.
    
    Conditions/exceptions apply; preferences remain preferences.
    
    # ALWAYS
    
    - Explicit constructor/initializer dependencies; inject DB, filesystem, network, collaborators. Native DI when available; resolve at composition roots/test setup. Centralize identifiers; separate production/test wiring; class types where supported, tokens for interfaces/config/ambiguity.
    - Container builds service graph, including tests; override changed IO/config. Small contracts for replaceable IO; concrete services when interfaces add no substitution value.
    - Deliberate lifetimes: infrastructure/intentional queues may be singletons; isolate per-execution context. Typed data-service facade optional when simplifying callers.
    - Thin controllers/commands/handlers/jobs; cohesive services; testable stateless calculation/formatting helpers. Existing lifecycle base class, otherwise native middleware/guards/interceptors; inheritance only for justified shared lifecycle.
    - Map differing DB/domain names, nullability, booleans, encodings at persistence boundary. Inject connection into data services; direct ORM services acceptable.
    - Preserve expected absence/rejection versus unexpected failure and existing result contracts. Validate untrusted entry input; useful error boundary, diagnostics, caller feedback. Promised atomic multi-write transition → datastore transaction.
    - Interactive entrypoints: meaningful progress/success/failure feedback. Localization optional unless requested/existing.
    - Colocated tests, project runner; observable outcomes + important failure paths. Persistence: prefer real services/queries, isolated DB, actual schema/migrations; ask SQLite versus project DB when choosing. In-memory DB only with compatible semantics; actual engine when constraints/transactions/dialect matter.
    - Explicit fixtures via reusable domain builders/seed helpers; reset mutable data per test, retain/reseed lookups. Shared resettable DB → sequential tests or separate parallel DBs.
    - Reuse test composition; override DB/external IO. Resolve subject through child container/native testing module; bind resolved instance only when assertions require identical instance. Testing modules: import relevant module, override external providers before compilation, then resolve.
    - Fake filesystem/messaging/remote APIs at boundaries; real subject/business collaborators. Pure dependency-free helpers may be instantiated directly.
    - Drive input boundary; assert responses/events/messages + persisted state. Relevant invalid input, missing data, rejection/cancellation, permissions covered.
    - Public lifecycle cleanup: queues, timers, spies/clocks, resources; singleton state needs separate reset. Native provider registration/tokens/overrides/lifecycle; native request/auth extension points.
    
    # NEVER
    
    - No business-logic global dependency lookup, injected service-locator container, second DI container, or parallel manually reconstructed test graph.
    - No request data in singleton fields; child container ≠ state isolation. Grouped facade ≠ transaction or mandatory layer.
    - No unnecessary IO interfaces, generic repositories, duplicate CRUD layers, or unjustified inheritance; no mandated language/ORM/DI/runner/framework.
    - No leaked persistence conversions/sensitive public error internals; infrastructure failure ≠ success.
    - No private-method/exact-call-sequence tests or private-collection cleanup. No handwritten RAM repositories replacing realistic persistence tests. DB row reset ≠ singleton reset.
    
    # Taste
    
    - Tabular/report data: preserve named fields through queries, preparation, templates and exports. Define columns by field name, with display/export order explicit; avoid discarding keys and matching values to headers/styles by numeric position. Preserve external positional formats at their serialization boundary.
    ✓ • 56ms

    local M = {}
    
    -- Positions are byte offsets, matching Neovim edits and selections.
    function M.tokens(text, engine)
        local tokens, i, incomplete, unicode_identifiers = {}, 1, false, false
        local function add(value, kind, first, last)
            tokens[#tokens + 1] = { value = value, upper = value:upper(), kind = kind, first = first, last = last }
        end
        while i <= #text do
            local first, char = i, text:sub(i, i)
            if char:match("%s") then
                i = i + 1
            elseif text:sub(i, i + 1) == "--" or engine and engine:match("^mysql") and char == "#" then
                i = (text:find("\n", i, true) or #text) + 1
            elseif text:sub(i, i + 1) == "/*" then
                local depth = 1
                i = i + 2
                while i <= #text and depth > 0 do
                    if text:sub(i, i + 1) == "/*" then
                        depth, i = depth + 1, i + 2
                    elseif text:sub(i, i + 1) == "*/" then
                        depth, i = depth - 1, i + 2
                    else
                        i = i + 1
                    end
                end
                incomplete = incomplete or depth > 0
            elseif char == "'" or char == '"' or char == "`" or char == "[" then
                local quote, value, closed = char == "[" and "]" or char, {}, false
                i = i + 1
                while i <= #text do
                    local next_char = text:sub(i, i)
                    if next_char == quote then
                        if text:sub(i + 1, i + 1) == quote then
                            value[#value + 1], i = quote, i + 2
                        else
                            i, closed = i + 1, true
                            break
                        end
                    elseif
                        next_char == "\\"
                        and char == "'"
                        and (engine == "mysql" or engine == "postgres" and text:sub(first - 1, first - 1):lower() == "e")
                    then
                        value[#value + 1], i = text:sub(i, i + 1), i + 2
                    else
                        value[#value + 1], i = next_char, i + 1
                    end
                end
                incomplete = incomplete or not closed
                add(table.concat(value), char == "'" and "string" or "identifier", first, i - 1)
            elseif char == "$" and text:sub(i):match("^%$[%w_]*%$") then
                local tag = text:sub(i):match("^%$[%w_]*%$")
                local finish = text:find(tag, i + #tag, true)
                incomplete = incomplete or not finish
                i = finish and finish + #tag or #text + 1
                add(text:sub(first, i - 1), "string", first, i - 1)
            elseif char:match("[%a_$]") or char:byte() >= 128 then
                local value = text:sub(i):match("^[%w_$\128-\255]+")
                unicode_identifiers = unicode_identifiers or value:find("[\128-\255]") ~= nil
                i = i + #value
                add(value, "word", first, i - 1)
            else
                add(char, "symbol", i, i)
                i = i + 1
            end
        end
        local legacy
        if unicode_identifiers then
            -- Preserve the old token identities so saved parameter values can migrate without resetting.
            legacy = {}
            for _, token in ipairs(tokens) do
                if token.kind == "word" and token.value:find("[\128-\255]") then
                    local at = token.first
                    while at <= token.last do
                        local char = text:sub(at, at)
                        local word = char:match("[%a_$]") and text:sub(at):match("^[%w_$]+")
                        local value = word or char
                        legacy[#legacy + 1] = {
                            kind = word and "word" or "symbol",
                            value = value,
                            upper = value:upper(),
                            first = at,
                            last = at + #value - 1,
                        }
                        at = at + #value
                    end
                else
                    legacy[#legacy + 1] = token
                end
            end
        end
        return tokens, incomplete, legacy
    end
    
    function M.statements(text, engine)
        local tokens, incomplete = M.tokens(text, engine)
        if incomplete then
            return nil, "SQL contains an unfinished quote or comment; select complete SQL explicitly"
        end
        local result, first, depth = {}, 1, 0
        for _, token in ipairs(tokens) do
            if token.kind == "symbol" and token.value == "(" then
                depth = depth + 1
            end
            if token.kind == "symbol" and token.value == ")" then
                depth = depth - 1
            end
            if token.kind == "symbol" and token.value == ";" and depth == 0 then
                local chunk = text:sub(first, token.first - 1)
                if #M.tokens(chunk, engine) > 0 then
                    result[#result + 1] = { sql = chunk, first = first, last = token.last }
                end
                first = token.last + 1
            end
        end
        if depth ~= 0 then
            return nil, "Unbalanced SQL parentheses; select complete SQL explicitly"
        end
        local tail = text:sub(first)
        if #M.tokens(tail, engine) > 0 then
            result[#result + 1] = { sql = tail, first = first, last = #text + 1 }
        end
        return result
    end
    
    function M.scope(text, offset, kind, selection, engine)
        if kind == "selection" then
            if not selection or selection == "" then
                return nil, "Select SQL first (character or line selection)"
            end
            return selection
        end
        if kind == "file" then
            return text
        end
        if kind == "section" then
            local first, last, position = 1, #text, 1
            local tokens = M.tokens(text, engine)
            for line in (text .. "\n"):gmatch("(.-)\n") do
                if line:match("^%s*%-%-%s*@section%s+") then
                    local inside = false
                    for _, token in ipairs(tokens) do
                        if token.first <= position and token.last >= position and token.kind == "string" then
                            inside = true
                        end
                    end
                    if not inside then
                        if position <= offset then
                            first = position
                        elseif position > offset then
                            last = position - 1
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
                position = position + #line + 1
            end
            return text:sub(first, last)
        end
        local statements, err = M.statements(text, engine)
        if not statements then
            return nil, err
        end
        for _, statement in ipairs(statements) do
            if offset >= statement.first and offset <= statement.last then
                return statement.sql
            end
        end
        return nil, "No statement at the SQL cursor"
    end
    
    local reserved = {}
    for word in
        ("SELECT FROM WHERE JOIN LEFT RIGHT INNER OUTER FULL CROSS ON USING GROUP ORDER HAVING LIMIT OFFSET UNION AS AND OR SET NATURAL WINDOW RETURNING FOR"):gmatch(
            "%S+"
        )
    do
        reserved[word] = true
    end
    
    local function identifier(token)
        return token and (token.kind == "identifier" or token.kind == "word" and not reserved[token.upper])
    end
    
    function M.table(metadata, name)
        local found
        for _, item in ipairs(metadata.tables or {}) do
            if item.key:lower() == name:lower() then
                return item
            end
            if item.name:lower() == name:lower() then
                if found then
                    return nil
                end
                found = item
            end
        end
        return found
    end

    package main
    
    import (
        "fmt"
        "regexp"
        "strings"
        "unicode"
    )
    
    type token struct {
        kind, text string
        start, end int
    }
    type statement struct {
        sql          string
        positions    []int
        confirmation bool
        autoSafe     bool
    }
    
    var word = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9$]*|^[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?`)
    var dollar = regexp.MustCompile(`^\$(?:[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*)?\$`)
    
    func lex(s, engine string) ([]token, error) {
        out := []token{}
        for i := 0; i < len(s); {
            start := i
            c := s[i : i+1]
            if unicode.IsSpace(rune(s[i])) {
                i++
                continue
            }
            if strings.HasPrefix(s[i:], "--") || strings.HasPrefix(engine, "mysql") && c == "#" {
                for i < len(s) && s[i] != '\n' {
                    i++
                }
                continue
            }
            if strings.HasPrefix(s[i:], "/*") {
                if strings.HasPrefix(s[i:], "/*!") || strings.HasPrefix(s[i:], "/*M!") {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Executable SQL comments are not supported")
                }
                depth := 1
                i += 2
                for i < len(s) && depth > 0 {
                    if strings.HasPrefix(s[i:], "/*") {
                        if engine != "postgres" {
                            return nil, fmt.Errorf("Nested SQL comments are unsupported in this dialect")
                        }
                        depth++
                        i += 2
                    } else if strings.HasPrefix(s[i:], "*/") {
                        depth--
                        i += 2
                    } else {
                        i++
                    }
                }
                if depth > 0 {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unfinished SQL comment")
                }
                continue
            }
            if engine == "postgres" {
                if tag := dollar.FindString(s[i:]); tag != "" {
                    end := strings.Index(s[i+len(tag):], tag)
                    if end < 0 {
                        return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unfinished dollar-quoted SQL")
                    }
                    i += len(tag)*2 + end
                    out = append(out, token{"string", s[start:i], start, i})
                    continue
                }
            }
            if strings.Contains("'\"`[", c) {
                close := c
                if c == "[" {
                    close = "]"
                }
                i++
                done := false
                for i < len(s) {
                    if s[i:i+1] == close {
                        i++
                        if i < len(s) && s[i:i+1] == close {
                            i++
                            continue
                        }
                        done = true
                        break
                    }
                    if s[i] == '\\' && c == "'" && (engine == "mysql" || engine == "postgres" && start > 0 && strings.EqualFold(s[start-1:start], "e")) {
                        i += 2
                    } else {
                        i++
                    }
                }
                if !done {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unfinished SQL quote")
                }
                kind := "identifier"
                if c == "'" {
                    kind = "string"
                }
                out = append(out, token{kind, s[start:i], start, i})
                continue
            }
            if w := word.FindString(s[i:]); w != "" {
                i += len(w)
                out = append(out, token{"word", w, start, i})
            } else {
                i++
                out = append(out, token{"symbol", c, start, i})
            }
        }
        return out, nil
    }
    
    func prepare(s, engine string) ([]statement, error) {
        if len(s) > 1024*1024 {
            return nil, fmt.Errorf("SQL must be text of at most 1 MiB")
        }
        tokens, err := lex(s, engine)
        if err != nil {
            return nil, err
        }
        pieces := []string{}
        first, depth := 0, 0
        for _, t := range tokens {
            if t.kind != "symbol" {
                continue
            }
            switch t.text {
            case "(":
                depth++
            case ")":
                depth--
                if depth < 0 {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unbalanced SQL parentheses")
                }
            case ";":
                if depth != 0 {
                    return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unsupported SQL statement body")
                }
                pieces = append(pieces, s[first:t.start])
                first = t.end
            }
        }
        if depth != 0 {
            return nil, fmt.Errorf("Unbalanced SQL parentheses")
        }
        pieces = append(pieces, s[first:])
        out := []statement{}
        for _, piece := range pieces {
            ts, _ := lex(piece, engine)
            if len(ts) == 0 {
                continue
            }
            st := statement{sql: piece, autoSafe: ts[0].kind == "word" && strings.EqualFold(ts[0].text, "SELECT")}
            // A nested WHERE does not limit the outer UPDATE or DELETE.
            level, mutation, where := 0, false, false
            for _, t := range ts {
                if t.kind == "symbol" {
                    if t.text == "(" {
                        level++
                    }
                    if t.text == ")" {
                        level--
                    }
                }
                if t.kind != "word" {
                    continue
                }
                w := strings.ToUpper(t.text)
                if strings.Contains(" INSERT UPDATE DELETE MERGE CREATE ALTER DROP TRUNCATE ATTACH DETACH PRAGMA COPY CALL DO SET RESET INTO LOCK UNLOCK GRANT REVOKE VACUUM ANALYZE ANALYSE LOAD ", " "+w+" ") {
                    st.autoSafe = false
                }
                if strings.Contains(" DROP TRUNCATE ", " "+w+" ") {
                    st.confirmation = true
                }

    func TestSQL(t *testing.T) {
        if _, err := prepare("SELECT 1 /* /* */ INTO OUTFILE 'x' -- */", "mysql"); err == nil {
            t.Fatal("nested comments bypassed the write guard")
        }
        if _, err := prepare("SELECT 1 /* outer /* inner */ outer */", "postgres"); err != nil {
            t.Fatal(err)
        }
        for _, s := range []string{"/*! SELECT 1 */", "SELECT 'unfinished", "SELECT (1", "SELECT ?1"} {
            if _, e := prepare(s, "mysql"); e == nil {
                t.Fatal("accepted", s)
            }
        }
        for _, tc := range []struct {
            sql                    string
            confirmation, autoSafe bool
        }{
            {"UPDATE customers SET name='x'", true, false},
            {"UPDATE customers SET name=(SELECT name FROM customers WHERE id=1)", true, false},
            {"DELETE FROM customers WHERE id=1", false, false},
            {"WITH x AS (SELECT 1) DELETE FROM customers", true, false},
            {"DROP TABLE customers", true, false},
            {"SELECT 1 INTO new_table", false, false},
            {"SELECT 'DELETE FROM customers'", false, true},
        } {
            statements, err := prepare(tc.sql, "sqlite")
            if err != nil || statements[0].confirmation != tc.confirmation || statements[0].autoSafe != tc.autoSafe {
                t.Fatalf("%s: %+v %v", tc.sql, statements, err)
            }
        }
        ss, e := prepare("SELECT ';?' AS literal; -- ?\nSELECT ?", "sqlite")
        if e != nil || len(ss) != 2 || len(ss[1].positions) != 1 {
            t.Fatal(ss, e)
        }
        ss, e = prepare("SELECT doc ? 'name', doc ?| array['x'], ? FROM things WHERE id = ?", "postgres")
        if e != nil || len(ss[0].positions) != 2 || !strings.Contains(boundSQL(ss[0], "postgres"), "$2") {
            t.Fatal(ss, e)
        }
        ss, e = prepare("SELECT $$?;$$, ?", "postgres")
        if e != nil || len(ss[0].positions) != 1 {
            t.Fatal(ss, e)
        }
        ss, e = prepare("SELECT '50%', ?, 100 % 7", "mysql")
        if e != nil || boundSQL(ss[0], "mysql") != ss[0].sql {
            t.Fatal(ss, e)
        }
        if _, e = prepare("SELECT 1 AS \"(\"", "sqlite"); e != nil {
            t.Fatal(e)
        }
    }

## User

> ¿Qué ocurre exactamente al cambiar de environment en editor mode con una pestaña de Query abierta? Por ejemplo, ¿desaparece una ventana, aparece una pestaña de otro proyecto o se muestra un error?

    local function terminal_windows(slot, include_tasks)
        local windows = {}
        for _, win in ipairs(vim.api.nvim_list_wins()) do
            local buf = vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(win)
            local owner = vim.b[buf].fox_env_slot
            if
                (owner == nil or owner == slot)
                and (include_tasks or not vim.b[buf].fox_is_task)
                and (vim.bo[buf].buftype == "terminal" or vim.b[buf].fox_is_multi_term)
            then
                windows[#windows + 1] = win
            end
        end
        return windows
    end
    
    --- Swaps global multi-terminal pool `_G._fox_terminals` in place with shallow clone.
    --- Avoids heavy deepcopies and GC pressure.
    --- @param target_pool table|nil
    local function swap_terminal_pool(target_pool)
        for _, win in ipairs(terminal_windows(M.get_active_slot(), true)) do
            pcall(vim.api.nvim_win_close, win, true)
        end
        _G._fox_terminals = _G._fox_terminals or {}
    
        for k in pairs(_G._fox_terminals) do
            _G._fox_terminals[k] = nil
        end
    
        if target_pool and type(target_pool) == "table" then
            for k, v in pairs(target_pool) do
                _G._fox_terminals[k] = v
            end
        end
    end

    function M.open()
        local state = M.state()
        M.draw_queries(state)
        M.select(state.query_files, { prompt = "Saved / open project queries" }, function(file)
            if file then
                M.new(file)
            end
        end)
    end
    
    function M.inline_buffer_visible(buf, win)
        return inline.owns(win, buf)
    end
    
    function M.close_inline_buffer(buf, win)
        if inline.owns(win, buf) then
            inline.detach(win)
        end
    end
    
    function M.open_split(command)
        if vim.t.fox_query_root then
            notify("Open an SQL split from the editor")
            return
        end
        local state, origin = M.state(), vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
        local files = {}
        for _, entry in ipairs(require("fox.query.files").list(state)) do
            if not entry.directory then
                files[#files + 1] = entry.relative
            end
        end
        if #files == 0 then
            notify("Create an SQL document in Query Mode first")
            return
        end
        M.select(files, { prompt = "QUERY / OPEN SQL TAB", split_style = true }, function(file)
            if not file or not valid(origin, "win") then
                return
            end
            vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(origin)
            if storage.root() ~= state.root then
                return
            end
            local buf = M.new(file, nil, false, true)
            if not buf then
                return
            end
            local split_command = command:gsub("vsplit$", "vnew"):gsub(" split$", " new")
            vim.cmd(split_command)
            local win = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
            local view = inline.attach(state, win, buf, bind_result_cells)
            vim.api.nvim_win_set_buf(win, buf)
            require("fox.ui.tabline").start_group(win, buf)
            local excluded = "r" .. storage.path(state.root, "")
            if not vim.tbl_contains(vim.opt.shada:get(), excluded) then
                vim.opt.shada:append(excluded)
            end
            M.restore_connection(view)
            M.refresh_view(view)
        end)
    end
    
    function M.profile(action)
        local state = run_state()
        local active = console(state)
        if action == "disconnect" then
            if active and active.client then
                active.client:close()

    --- Captures the in-memory state of the currently active environment WITHOUT
    --- tearing down any UI. This is a fast, zero-I/O snapshot used during hot
    --- environment switches. Environment state never writes to disk.
    --- @param env table|nil
    --- @param terminal_pending boolean|nil
    --- @return boolean ok
    local function snapshot_active_environment(env, terminal_pending)
        env = env or M.get_active_environment()
        if not env then
            return false
        end
    
        M.update_current_directory()
    
        -- Remember which buffer the user was editing so we can restore it on switch-back
        env.last_window = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
        local cur_buf = vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf()
        if vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(cur_buf) and vim.bo[cur_buf].buftype == "" then
            local buf_name = vim.api.nvim_buf_get_name(cur_buf)
            if buf_name ~= "" then
                env.last_buffer = buf_name
                env.last_buffer_nr = cur_buf
            end
        end
    
        -- Save the selected terminal slot so each environment has independent terminal selection
        env.selected_terminal = _G._fox_selected_terminal or 1
        env.terminal_open = terminal_pending or #terminal_windows(env.slot) > 0
    
        -- Capture state in memory (0 disk I/O, 0 UI teardown, shallow terminal copy)
        env.updated_at = os.time()
    
        local term_pool = {}
        if _G._fox_terminals then
            for k, v in pairs(_G._fox_terminals) do
                term_pool[k] = v
            end
        end
        it("resolves aliases and distinguishes output/join/filter columns", function()

    function M.focus_results(view)
        view.results_hidden = false
        M.refresh(view)
        if view.result_win then
            vim.cmd.stopinsert()
            vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(view.result_win)
            return true
        end
        return false
    end
    
    function M.attach(workspace, win, buf, bind_result_cells)
        local view = {
            inline = true,
            workspace = workspace,
            root = workspace.root,
            active = buf,
            bind_result_cells = bind_result_cells,
            sql_win = win,
            consoles = workspace.consoles,
            connection_status = workspace.connection_status,
            metadata = {},
        }
        views[win] = view
        return view
    end
    
    function M.detach(win)
        local view = views[win]
        if not view then
            return
        end
        views[win] = nil
        hide(view)
        ui.close(view.result_popup)
        if view.result_buf and vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(view.result_buf) then
            vim.api.nvim_buf_delete(view.result_buf, { force = true })
        end
    end

        				if candidate ~= win and vim.api.nvim_win_get_config(candidate).relative == "" and not views[candidate] then
        					anchor = candidate
        					break
        				end
        			end
        			if anchor then
        				local pos, other = vim.fn.win_screenpos(win), vim.fn.win_screenpos(anchor)
        				local split = pos[1] == other[1] and (pos[2] < other[2] and "left" or "right")
        					or (pos[1] < other[1] and "above" or "below")
        				resume[#resume + 1] = { view = view, anchor = anchor, split = split }
        				views[win] = nil
        				vim.api.nvim_win_close(win, true)
        			else
        				resume[#resume + 1] = { view = view }
        				local placeholder = vim.api.nvim_create_buf(false, false)
        				vim.bo[placeholder].bufhidden = "wipe"
        				vim.api.nvim_win_set_buf(win, placeholder)
        			end
        		end
        	end
        	return resume
        end
        
        function M.enter_environment(resume)
        	for _, entry in ipairs(resume or {}) do
        		local view = entry.view
        		if entry.anchor then
        			local anchor = vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(entry.anchor) and entry.anchor or vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
        			local win = vim.api.nvim_open_win(view.active, false, { win = anchor, split = entry.split })
        			view.sql_win, views[win] = win, view
        			require("fox.ui.tabline").start_group(win, view.active)
        		end
        		if views[view.sql_win] == view and attached(view) then
        			vim.api.nvim_win_set_buf(view.sql_win, view.active)
        			M.refresh(view)
        		end
        	end
        end

        if manager and not manager.is_paused then

    local function poll()
        local buf = vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf()
        if not timer or polling or vim.bo[buf].buftype ~= "terminal" then
            return
        end
        if last_poll[buf] and vim.uv.now() - last_poll[buf] < 3000 then
            return
        end
        last_poll[buf] = vim.uv.now()
        local channel = vim.b[buf].terminal_job_id
        local ok, pid = pcall(vim.fn.jobpid, channel or -1)
        if not ok or pid <= 0 then
            agents[buf] = nil
            return
        end
        local windows = vim.fn.has("win32") == 1
        local command = windows
                and {
                    "powershell.exe",
                    "-NoLogo",
                    "-NoProfile",
                    "-NonInteractive",
                    "-Command",
                    "[Console]::OutputEncoding = [System.Text.UTF8Encoding]::new(); Get-CimInstance Win32_Process | Select-Object ProcessId,ParentProcessId,Name,CommandLine | ConvertTo-Json -Compress",
                }
            or { "ps", "-eo", "pid=,ppid=,comm=,args=" }
        polling = true
        local started = pcall(
            vim.system,
            command,
            { text = true, timeout = 10000 },
            vim.schedule_wrap(function(result)
                polling = false
                if not vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(buf) or vim.b[buf].terminal_job_id ~= channel then
                    return
                end
                if result.code ~= 0 then
                    agents[buf] = nil
                    if vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf() == buf then
                        refresh()
                    end
                    return
                end
                local processes = {}
                if windows then
                    local decoded, rows = pcall(vim.json.decode, result.stdout)
                    if not decoded or type(rows) ~= "table" then
                        return
                    end
                    if rows.ProcessId then
                        rows = { rows }
                    end
                    for _, row in ipairs(rows) do
                        processes[#processes + 1] =
                            { pid = row.ProcessId, ppid = row.ParentProcessId, name = row.Name or "", command = row.CommandLine }
                    end
                else
                    for line in result.stdout:gmatch("[^\n]+") do
                        local child, parent, name, args = line:match("^%s*(%d+)%s+(%d+)%s+(%S+)%s*(.*)$")
                        if child then
                            processes[#processes + 1] = { pid = tonumber(child), ppid = tonumber(parent), name = name, command = args }
                        end
                    end
                end
                local agent = M.find_agent(processes, pid)
                local previous = agents[buf]
                agents[buf] = agent and { name = agent, channel = channel } or nil
                if vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf() == buf and (previous and previous.name or nil) ~= agent then
                    refresh()
                end
            end)
        )
        if not started then
            polling = false
        end
    end
    
    local function ai_activity()
        local buf = vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf()
        local agent = agents[buf]
        if vim.bo[buf].buftype == "terminal" and agent and agent.channel == vim.b[buf].terminal_job_id then
            local index = (math.floor(os.time() / 15) + buf) % #messages + 1
            if rotation.message ~= index then
                local icon = math.random(#fox_icons - (rotation.icon and 1 or 0))
                if rotation.icon and icon >= rotation.icon then
                    icon = icon + 1
                end
                rotation = { message = index, icon = icon }
            end
            return messages[index] .. "...", fox_icons_url .. fox_icons[rotation.icon] .. ".png"
        end
    local M = {}
    local names = {}
    for _, name in ipairs(require("fox.discord.ai_cli_names")) do
        if name ~= "cmd" or vim.fn.has("win32") == 0 then
            names[name] = true
        end
    end
    local messages = require("fox.discord.loading_msgs")
    local git_icon_url =

    local fox_icons_url =

        "fox-arctic-looking-straight",

    local rotation = {}

    local project = require("fox.core.project")

    -- Only inspect loaded dashboards; presence must never load Git UI modules.

        local win = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
        local center = package.loaded["plugins.fox.git.git_center.config"]
        local root = center and center.get_active_target() or nil
        root = root and root.full_path or nil
        if vim.bo.filetype == "fox_git_diff_menu" then
            return "Selecting how to review project...", vim.b.fox_git_root or project.root()
        end
        local log = package.loaded["plugins.fox.git.log_diff"]
        if log and log.state.is_open then
            for _, key in ipairs({ "files_win", "logs_win", "before_win", "after_win" }) do
                if log.state[key] == win then
                    return "Reviewing commit log...", log.state.cwd
                end
            end
        end
        if center then
            for _, key in ipairs({
                "main_win",
                "preview_win",
                "tab_win",
                "diff_modal_win",
                "log_win",
                "log_right_win",
                "branch_win",
                "graph_win",
                "graph_right_win",
            }) do
                if center[key] == win then
                    return "Gitting around...", root or vim.fn.getcwd()
                end
            end
        end
        local diff = package.loaded["plugins.fox.git.diff_mode"]
        local state = diff and diff.state
        if state and state.is_active then
            for _, key in ipairs({ "file_list_win", "editor_win", "dual_left_win", "dual_right_win" }) do
                if state[key] == win then
                    local text = "Reviewing Working Tree vs HEAD"
                    if state.mode == "between_branches" then
                        text = "Reviewing a whole branch..."
                    elseif state.custom_commits then
                        text = "Reviewing selected commits..."
                    elseif state.base_ref ~= "HEAD" and state.commits_behind == 0 then
                        text = "Reviewing a whole branch..."
                    elseif state.commits_behind > 0 then
                        text = "Reviewing last " .. state.commits_behind .. (state.commits_behind == 1 and " commit" or " commits")
                    end
                    return text, state.cwd
                end
            end
        end
    end
    
    function M.privacy(root)
        local file, exists = project.config_path("discord.json", root)
        if not exists then
    -- ============================================================================
    -- FOX PLUGIN: Environments -- Multi-Project Workspace Container (Slots 1..9)
    -- ============================================================================
    -- WHAT IT DOES
    --   1. Manages up to 9 concurrent, isolated project environments inside a single
    --      Neovim instance without running multiple separate windows/processes.
    --   2. Switch instantly between project cwds with <C-S-1>..<C-S-9> (and terminal
    --      compat aliases <C-!>..<C-(>).
    --   3. Provides a full interactive CRUD menu (<C-S-e> or :EnvironmentMenu) to
    --      list, switch, create, rename, and close environment slots.
    --   4. Scopes LSP servers per environment:
    --      - On environment close: automatically stops all LSP clients whose root_dir
    --        belongs to that closed environment.
    --      - On environment switch: keeps dormant LSPs intact in memory (0% CPU)
    --        instead of killing and cold-booting language servers.
    --   5. Scopes terminals per environment:
    --      - Each environment owns its own 9 terminal slots (<A-1>..<A-9>).
    --      - Background terminal jobs keep running in their respective project roots.
    --      - Closing an environment terminates its scoped terminal jobs.
    --   6. Bufferline & UI Isolation:
    --      - Bufferline tabs only show files belonging to the active environment.
    --      - A discreet statusline indicator shows `󰒋 [Env N: name]` ONLY when > 1
    --        environments are active. When only 1 environment is active, nothing is shown.
    --   7. Session-only environments & Workspaces Compatibility:
    --      - Environment state lives only in RAM until Neovim exits.
    --      - Can export any environment slot as a named Workspace (:EnvironmentSaveAsWorkspace)
    --        or load any saved Workspace into a slot (:EnvironmentLoadWorkspace).
    -- ============================================================================
    
    local lazy_req = require("fox.core.lazy_require")
    local path = lazy_req("fox.core.path")
    
    local M = {}
    
    -- ============================================================================
    -- CONFIGURATION
    -- ============================================================================
    
    M.settings = {
        --- Title on every notification from this module.
        notify_title = "FOX Environments",
    
        --- Maximum number of environment slots (1..9 for numeric pad/number row).
        max_slots = 9,
    
        --- Filetypes treated as transient UI during environment switches.
        transient_filetypes = {
            "TaskRunner",
            "toggleterm",
            "neo-tree",
            "alpha",
            "dashboard",
            "FoxGitCenter",
            "FoxConflict",
            "FoxConflictResult",
            "FoxLogDiff",
            "FoxDiffSidebar",
            "FoxDiffMode",
            "gitcommit",
        },
    
        keys = {
            --- Open the Environments CRUD menu.
            menu = { "<C-S-e>", "<C-S-E>", "<leader>ee" },
            --- Quick slot selection prefix: the slot number is appended (<C-S-1>..<C-S-9>).
            slot_prefix = "<C-S-",
            --- Terminal-compatible symbol prefix when Shift+1..9 produces symbols under Ctrl.
            symbols = { "!", "@", "#", "$", "%", "^", "&", "*", "(" },
        },
    }
    
    -- Fast O(1) transient lookup set (avoiding linear table scans and GC allocations)
    local transient_set = {}
    for _, ft in ipairs(M.settings.transient_filetypes) do
        transient_set[ft] = true
    end
    
    -- ============================================================================
    -- STATE (Global for reload survival and cross-module inspection)
    -- ============================================================================
    
    _G._fox_environments = _G._fox_environments or {}
    _G._fox_active_env_slot = _G._fox_active_env_slot or 1
    
    -- Module-level fast caches
    local _normalized_cwds = {}
    local _active_count = 0
    local _has_multiple_envs = false
    local _cached_indicator = ""
    local _cached_indicator_slot = nil
    
    --- Synchronizes internal cached metrics for sub-millisecond query lookups.
    local function sync_internal_state()
        local count = 0
        _normalized_cwds = {}
        for slot = 1, M.settings.max_slots do
            local env = _G._fox_environments[slot]
            if env ~= nil then
                count = count + 1
                if env.cwd and env.cwd ~= "" then
                    _normalized_cwds[slot] = path.normalize(env.cwd)
                end
            end
        end
        _active_count = count
        _has_multiple_envs = (count > 1)
        _cached_indicator = ""
        _cached_indicator_slot = nil
    end
    
    -- ============================================================================
    -- NOTIFICATIONS
    -- ============================================================================
    
    --- Notification helper.
    ✓ • 32ms

    local function visible_buffers(candidates, win)
        local selected, ordinary, previews, sources = {}, {}, {}, {}
        for _, buf in ipairs(candidates) do
            if M.visible(buf, win) and not sources[source(buf)] then
                sources[source(buf)] = true
                local list = M.is_pinned(buf) and selected or vim.b[buf].fox_preview_owner and previews or ordinary
                list[#list + 1] = buf
            end
        end
        return vim.list_extend(vim.list_extend(selected, ordinary), previews)
    end
    function M.buffers(win)

            return visible_buffers(window_groups[win] or {}, win)

        local seen, valid = {}, {}
        for _, buf in ipairs(order) do
            if vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(buf) then

    local function code_windows()
        local windows = {}
        for _, win in ipairs(vim.api.nvim_tabpage_list_wins(0)) do
            if code_window(win) then
                windows[#windows + 1] = win
            end
        end
        return windows
    end
    
    local function remember_window(win)
        if not code_window(win) then
            return
        end
        local buf = vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(win)
        local group = window_groups[win] or {}
        window_groups[win] = group
        if vim.bo[buf].buflisted and vim.api.nvim_buf_get_name(buf) ~= "" and not vim.tbl_contains(group, buf) then
            group[#group + 1] = buf
        end
    end
    
    local function group_window()
        local tab = vim.api.nvim_get_current_tabpage()
        if vim.t[tab].fox_query_root then
            return nil
        end
        local current = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
        local windows = code_windows()
        if #windows > 1 then
            grouped_pages[tab] = true
        end
        if grouped_pages[tab] then
            return code_window(current) and current or windows[1]
        end
    end
    local icon_styles = {}
    local function source(buf)
        return vim.b[buf].fox_media_source or buf
    end
    local function escape(value)
        return tostring(value):gsub("%%", "%%%%"):gsub("[\r\n]", " ")
    end
    local function hl(name)
        return "%#" .. name .. "#"
    end
    local function clip(text, width)
        if vim.fn.strdisplaywidth(text) <= width then
            return text
        end
        if width <= 0 then
            return ""
        end
        local result = ""
        for i = 0, vim.fn.strchars(text) - 1 do
            local char = vim.fn.strcharpart(text, i, 1)
            if vim.fn.strdisplaywidth(result .. char .. "…") > width then
                break
            end
            result = result .. char
        end
        return result .. "…"
    end
    M.clip = clip

Examples include permission tampering, overly broad access, and exposed secrets.\n- Broad, persistent, or production-impacting weakening of a shared service or sensitive boundary is `high` risk.\n- Temporarily disabling a narrowly scoped cert check, monitor, or similar control for a task-specific bounded action is usually `medium`. These actions are `high` risk.\n- Do not treat normal internal operational workflows as destructive solely because they are large-scale or long-running unless there is evidence of damage, service disruption, or export outside approved systems.\n- Do not assume the user has version control when evaluating file changes for destructiveness.     local t = require("fox.lib.foxnvim.test")
    
    t.describe("Query SQL editor split", function()
        t.it("executes a saved SQL tab and restores its results and full Query popups across environments", function()
            require("lazy").load({ plugins = { "fox_query", "fox_tabline" } })
            local query, storage = require("fox.query"), require("fox.query.storage")
            local inline, tabs = require("fox.query.inline"), require("fox.ui.tabline")
            local environments = require("plugins.fox.tools.environments")
            local cwd, shada = vim.fn.getcwd(), vim.o.shada
            local root, other = vim.fn.tempname(), vim.fn.tempname()
            vim.fn.mkdir(root, "p")
            vim.fn.mkdir(other, "p")
            vim.api.nvim_set_current_dir(root)
            local first = environments.get_active_slot()
            local second = first == 2 and 3 or 2
            environments.create_environment(second, other, "Other", false)
            local editor_tab, editor = vim.api.nvim_get_current_tabpage(), vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
    
            local function key(lhs)
                local mapping = vim.fn.maparg(lhs, "n", false, true)
                assert(mapping.callback, "missing mapping " .. lhs)
                mapping.callback()
            end
            local function wait_until(predicate, message)
                assert(vim.wait(5000, predicate, 10), message)
            end
    
            local ok, err = xpcall(function()
                local fixture = vim.fn.stdpath("config") .. "/tests/query/fixture.db"
                assert(vim.uv.fs_copyfile(fixture, root .. "/fixture.db"))
                assert(storage.save(root, "connections.json", {
                    profiles = { { id = "local", engine = "sqlite", path = "fixture.db" } },
                }))
                assert(
                    storage.write(
                        root,
                        "queries/example.sql",
                        "SELECT name, id FROM things WHERE id = 1 UNION ALL SELECT 'second', 2;\n\nSELECT 42;"
                    )
                )
                local sql = query.new("example.sql", "sqlite", false)
                local origin = vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf()
                key("<C-S-l>")
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_buf_get_lines(0, 6, 7, false)[1]).toContain("[3]   SQL Tab")
                key("3")
                key("<CR>")
                local view = assert(inline.current())
                local owner = view.sql_win
                local item = query.state().consoles[sql]
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_tabpage()).toBe(editor_tab)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(editor)).toBe(origin)
                t.expect(tabs.buffers(owner)).toEqual({ sql })
                t.expect(#vim.api.nvim_tabpage_list_wins(editor_tab)).toBe(2)
                wait_until(function()
                    return item.metadata and item.client.connected and not item.client.busy
                end, "SQLite metadata unavailable")
                local lines = vim.api.nvim_buf_get_lines(sql, 0, -1, false)
                local first_statement = assert(vim.fn.search("SELECT name", "w"))
                local second_statement = assert(vim.fn.search("SELECT 42", "w"))
                local namespace = vim.api.nvim_get_namespaces().fox_query_statement
                local function check_outline(row)
                    vim.api.nvim_win_set_cursor(owner, { row, 0 })
                    vim.api.nvim_exec_autocmds("CursorMoved", { buffer = sql })
                    wait_until(function()
                        local marks = vim.api.nvim_buf_get_extmarks(sql, namespace, 0, -1, { details = true })
                        return #marks == 2
                            and marks[1][2] == row - 1
                            and marks[2][2] == row - 1
                            and marks[1][4].virt_lines_above
                            and marks[2][4].virt_lines ~= nil
                    end, "Statement outline did not follow the SQL cursor")
                end
                check_outline(first_statement)
                check_outline(second_statement)
                check_outline(first_statement)
                local completion = require("fox.query.completion").new()
                local function labels()
                    local names = {}
                    completion:get_completions({ bufnr = sql, cursor = { first_statement, 8 } }, function(result)
                        for _, entry in ipairs(result.items) do
                            names[entry.label] = true
                        end
                    end)
                    return names
                end
                t.expect(labels().name).toBe(true)
                local workspace = query.state()
                local metadata = workspace.metadata
                workspace.metadata = { tables = { { key = "unrelated", name = "unrelated" } }, relations = {} }
                vim.api.nvim_buf_set_lines(sql, -1, -1, false, { "-- " .. string.rep("x", 129 * 1024) })
                local large = labels()
                t.expect(large.SELECT).toBe(true)
                t.expect(large.things).toBe(true)
                t.expect(large.unrelated).toBe(nil)
                vim.api.nvim_buf_set_lines(sql, 0, -1, false, lines)
                workspace.metadata = metadata
                vim.api.nvim_win_set_cursor(owner, { first_statement, 0 })
    
                key("<C-CR>")
                wait_until(function()
                    return view.result_win and item.result
                end, "Bottom results did not open")
                t.expect(item.result.sets[1].rows[1]).toEqual({ "fox", "1" })
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_config(view.result_win).win).toBe(owner)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_config(view.result_win).anchor).toBe("SW")
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()).toBe(owner)
                vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(editor)
                key("<C-l>")
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()).toBe(owner)
                key("<C-j>")
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()).toBe(view.result_win)
                key("J")
                local first_cell = vim.api.nvim_win_get_cursor(0)
                key("L")
                local next_cell = vim.api.nvim_win_get_cursor(0)
                t.expect(next_cell[1]).toBe(first_cell[1])
                t.expect(next_cell[2] > first_cell[2]).toBe(true)
                key("J")
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_cursor(0)[1]).toBe(first_cell[1] + 1)
                key("K")
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_cursor(0)).toEqual(next_cell)
                key("H")
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_cursor(0)).toEqual(first_cell)
                key("<C-k>")
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()).toBe(owner)
                key("<C-S-CR>")
                wait_until(function()
                    return view.result_popup and vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(view.result_popup)
                end, "Large results popup did not open")
                local popup = view.result_popup
                assert(environments.switch_environment(second))
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_config(popup).hide).toBe(true)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(owner) == sql).toBe(false)
                assert(environments.switch_environment(first))
                t.expect(not vim.api.nvim_win_get_config(popup).hide).toBe(true)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()).toBe(popup)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(owner)).toBe(sql)
                key("q")
                key("gC")
                local picker = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
                assert(environments.switch_environment(second))
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_get_config(picker).hide).toBe(true)
                assert(environments.switch_environment(first))
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()).toBe(picker)
                key("q")
    
                query.show()
                local state = query.state()
                query.results_popup()
                local query_popup = assert(state.result_popup)
                assert(environments.switch_environment(second))
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_tabpage() == state.tab).toBe(false)
                assert(environments.switch_environment(first))
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_tabpage()).toBe(state.tab)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()).toBe(query_popup)
                t.expect(state.mode).toBe("query")
                query.hide()
                vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(owner)
                vim.cmd.FoxQ()
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(owner)).toBe(false)
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(sql)).toBe(true)
                t.expect(not vim.t.fox_query_root).toBe(true)
    
                -- Recover a pane left on Query's placeholder by an older close path.
                vim.cmd("rightbelow vsplit")
                local stranded = vim.api.nvim_get_current_win()
                local placeholder = query.empty(state)
                vim.api.nvim_win_set_buf(stranded, placeholder)
                vim.cmd.FoxQ()
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(stranded)).toBe(false)
    
                query.show()
                vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(state.sql_win)
                vim.cmd.write()
                vim.cmd.FoxQ()
                t.expect(vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(state.sql_win)).toBe(true)

                    vim.bo[buf].modified = false
                end
            end
            query.close_root(root)
            vim.api.nvim_set_current_dir(cwd)

    local function is_valid_win(win)
        return type(win) == "number" and vim.api.nvim_win_is_valid(win)
    end
    
    --- @param buf integer|nil
    --- @return boolean
    local function is_valid_buf(buf)
        return type(buf) == "number" and vim.api.nvim_buf_is_valid(buf)
    end
    
    --- True when bufnr belongs to a terminal.
    --- @param bufnr integer|nil
    --- @return boolean
    local function is_term_buf(bufnr)
        return is_valid_buf(bufnr) and (vim.bo[bufnr].buftype == "terminal" or vim.b[bufnr].fox_is_multi_term)
    end
    
    --- True when win displays a terminal buffer.
    --- @param win integer|nil
    --- @return boolean
    local function is_term_win(win)
        if not is_valid_win(win) then
            return false
        end
        local buf = vim.api.nvim_win_get_buf(win)
        return is_term_buf(buf)
    end
    
    --- Slot record for terminal `n`, created on demand.
    --- @param n integer
    --- @return table slot `{ buf, win }`
    local function get_term(n)
        terminals[n] = terminals[n] or { buf = nil, win = nil }
        return terminals[n]
    end
    
    --- Claims `bufnr` for the first free slot, tagging the buffer with its number.
    --- @param bufnr integer
    --- @param winid integer|nil Window showing the buffer, when it has one.
    --- @return integer|nil slot
    local function adopt_buffer(bufnr, winid)
        for n = 1, M.settings.count do
            local t = get_term(n)
            if not is_valid_buf(t.buf) then
                t.buf = bufnr
                t.win = winid or t.win
                vim.b[bufnr].fox_term_num = n
                vim.b[bufnr].fox_is_multi_term = true
                -- Tag with the active environment slot for isolation
                local env_slot = _G._fox_active_env_slot or 1
                vim.b[bufnr].fox_env_slot = env_slot
                return n
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    
    --- True for a terminal buffer this plugin may own (task outputs are excluded).
    --- Also excludes terminals tagged to a different environment slot.
    --- @param bufnr integer
    --- @return boolean
    local function is_adoptable(bufnr)
        if vim.bo[bufnr].buftype ~= "terminal" or vim.b[bufnr].fox_is_task then
            return false
        end
        -- If the buffer is tagged to a different environment, don't adopt it
        local buf_env_slot = vim.b[bufnr].fox_env_slot
        if buf_env_slot ~= nil then
            local active_env_slot = _G._fox_active_env_slot or 1
            if buf_env_slot ~= active_env_slot then
                return false
            end
        end
        return true
    end
    
    --- Reconciles the slot table with reality: drops dead handles, re-attaches
    --- tagged buffers, and adopts untagged terminals (from a session or `:terminal`).
    --- Respects environment scoping: only syncs terminals belonging to the active environment.
    local function sync_terminals()
        local active_env_slot = _G._fox_active_env_slot or 1
    
        for n = 1, M.settings.count do
            local t = terminals[n]
            if t then
                t.win = is_valid_win(t.win) and t.win or nil
    
    --- `:terminal` command for the current project, routed through WSL when the
    --- project lives on a WSL path.
    --- @return string cmd
    local function terminal_open_cmd()
        local ok, wsl = pcall(require, "plugins.fox.tools.wsl")
        local wsl_cmd = ok and wsl.shell_command_for_cwd(vim.fn.getcwd()) or nil
        if wsl_cmd then
            return "terminal " .. wsl_cmd
        end
        -- Kitty starts the user's interactive shell; use the same initialization
        -- inside Neovim while keeping Bash for POSIX tool/task commands.
        local shell = vim.env.SHELL
        if vim.fn.has("unix") == 1 and shell and vim.fn.executable(shell) == 1 then
            return "terminal " .. vim.fn.fnameescape(shell) .. " -il"
        end
        return "terminal"
    end
    
    --- Shows terminal `n` in `win`, spawning the shell when the slot has no buffer.
    --- @param t table Slot record.
    --- @param n integer Slot number.
    --- @param win integer Window to fill.
    local function fill_window(t, n, win)
        if is_valid_win(win) then
            vim.api.nvim_set_current_win(win)
        end
    
        if is_valid_buf(t.buf) then
            vim.api.nvim_win_set_buf(win, t.buf)
            return
        end
    
        local cwd = vim.fn.getcwd()
        local stat_fn = (vim.uv or vim.loop).fs_stat
        if stat_fn(cwd .. "/.foxnvim/secondary_repos.json") then
            local ok_sec, sec = pcall(require, "fox.git.secondary")
            if ok_sec and sec then
                sec.setup_environment(cwd)
            end
        end
    
        vim.cmd(terminal_open_cmd())
        t.buf = vim.api.nvim_get_current_buf()
        vim.bo[t.buf].buflisted = false
        vim.b[t.buf].fox_term_num = n
        vim.b[t.buf].fox_is_multi_term = true
        vim.b[t.buf].fox_env_slot = _G._fox_active_env_slot or 1
    end

    if hook_event_name and hook_event_name != "SessionStart":
        raise SystemExit(0)

    #!/bin/sh
    # installed by herdr
    # managed by herdr; reinstalling or updating the integration overwrites this file.
    # add custom hooks beside this file instead of editing it.
    # HERDR_INTEGRATION_ID=antigravity_cli
    # HERDR_INTEGRATION_VERSION=2
    
    # Session-only: this hook reports the Antigravity conversation so Herdr can
    # resume the pane. Lifecycle state comes from Herdr's screen detection.
    
    set -eu
    
    # Antigravity CLI expects a JSON object on stdout and this hook never injects
    # anything, so every exit path emits an empty object.
    emit_and_exit() {
      printf '{}\n'
      exit 0
    }
    
    [ "${1:-}" = "session" ] || emit_and_exit
    [ "${HERDR_ENV:-}" = "1" ] || emit_and_exit
    [ -n "${HERDR_SOCKET_PATH:-}" ] || emit_and_exit
    [ -n "${HERDR_PANE_ID:-}" ] || emit_and_exit

    import json
    import os
    import socket
    import sys
    import time
    
    try:
        payload = json.load(sys.stdin)
    except Exception:
        raise SystemExit(0)
    
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise SystemExit(0)
    
    def text(name):
        value = payload.get(name)
        return value if isinstance(value, str) and value else None
    
    session_id = text("conversationId")
    if session_id is None:
        raise SystemExit(0)
    
    seq = time.time_ns()
    params = {
        "pane_id": os.environ["HERDR_PANE_ID"],
        "source": "herdr:antigravity_cli",
        "agent": "agy",
        "seq": seq,
        "agent_session_id": session_id,
    }
    
    transcript_path = text("transcriptPath")
    if transcript_path is not None:
        params["agent_session_path"] = transcript_path
    
    request = json.dumps({
        "id": f"herdr:antigravity_cli:{seq}",
        "method": "pane.report_agent_session",
        "params": params,
    })
    try:
        with socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as client:
            client.settimeout(0.5)
            client.connect(os.environ["HERDR_SOCKET_PATH"])
            client.sendall((request + "\n").encode())
            client.recv(4096)
    except Exception:

    const AGENT = "opencode";
    const ROUTE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 100;
    const SELECTION_RETRY_DELAYS_MS = [100, 400, 1_000];
    
    function requestOnce(sessionID) {
      const paneId = process.env.HERDR_PANE_ID;
      const socketPath = process.env.HERDR_SOCKET_PATH;
      if (!paneId || !socketPath) {
        return Promise.resolve();
      }
    
      const socketEndpoint =
        process.platform === "win32" ? `\\\\.\\pipe\\${socketPath}` : socketPath;
      const request = {
        id: `${SOURCE}:tui:${Date.now()}:${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1_000_000)
          .toString()
          .padStart(6, "0")}`,
        method: "pane.report_agent_session",
        params: {
          pane_id: paneId,
          source: SOURCE,
          agent: AGENT,
          agent_session_id: sessionID,
          session_start_source: "select",
        },
      };
    
      return new Promise((resolve) => {
        const client = net.createConnection(socketEndpoint, () => {
          client.write(`${JSON.stringify(request)}\n`);
        });
        const finish = () => {
          client.destroy();
          resolve();
        };
    
        client.setTimeout(500, finish);
        client.on("data", finish);
        client.on("error", finish);
        client.on("end", finish);
        client.on("close", resolve);
      });
    }
    
    export default {
      id: "herdr.opencode.session-selection",
      tui: async (api) => {
        if (
          process.env.HERDR_ENV !== "1" ||
          !process.env.HERDR_SOCKET_PATH ||
          !process.env.HERDR_PANE_ID
        ) {
          return;
        }
    
        let selectedSessionID;
        let retryIndex = 0;
        let nextReportAt = 0;
        let reportPending = false;
        const syncSelectedSession = async () => {
          const route = api.route.current;
          const sessionID = route?.name === "session" ? route.params?.sessionID : undefined;
          const session =
            typeof sessionID === "string" && sessionID
              ? api.state.session.get(sessionID)
              : undefined;
          if (!session || session.parentID) {
            selectedSessionID = undefined;
            retryIndex = 0;
            nextReportAt = 0;
            return;
          }
          if (sessionID !== selectedSessionID) {
            selectedSessionID = sessionID;
            retryIndex = 0;
            nextReportAt = 0;
          }
          if (reportPending || Date.now() < nextReportAt) {
            return;
          }
    
          const reportingSessionID = sessionID;
          reportPending = true;
          try {
            await requestOnce(reportingSessionID);
          } catch {
            // Best-effort reporting retries below while the selected route remains active.
          } finally {
            reportPending = false;
          }
          if (selectedSessionID !== reportingSessionID) {
            retryIndex = 0;
            nextReportAt = 0;
            return;
          }
          const retryDelay = SELECTION_RETRY_DELAYS_MS[retryIndex];
          retryIndex += 1;
          nextReportAt = retryDelay === undefined ? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY : Date.now() + retryDelay;
        };
    
        await syncSelectedSession();
        const routePoll = setInterval(() => void syncSelectedSession(), ROUTE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS);
        api.lifecycle.onDispose(() => clearInterval(routePoll));
      },
    };

## Assistant

Revisé Herdr: **restaura la conversación usando el ID de sesión de cada agente**, no conserva el proceso después de reiniciar la computadora. Al volver a abrir el proyecto, inicia nuevamente el CLI con su comando de reanudación. [Documentación de sesiones de Herdr](https://herdr.dev/tui/docs/session-state/)

Para obtener los IDs, Herdr instala integraciones específicas: hooks para Codex y Claude, un plugin para OpenCode y un hook para agy. En agy obtiene el ID después de enviar el primer mensaje. Sus comandos de reanudación incluyen `codex resume <id>`, `claude --resume <id>`, `opencode --session <id>` y `agy --conversation <id>`. [Integraciones de Herdr](https://herdr.dev/tui/docs/integrations/)

También comprobé que tienes Herdr instalado, pero ninguna de esas cuatro integraciones está instalada. Las estudié en un directorio temporal, sin modificar tus configuraciones.

La restauración equivalente en FoxVim **todavía no está implementada**. Este mecanismo permite hacerla sin tmux y recuperar el chat tras un reinicio, aunque el proceso y el estado temporal de la terminal serán nuevos.

## User

implementalo

## Assistant

Voy a implementar la captura del ID por terminal y su restauración por proyecto, siguiendo las integraciones que revisé. Mantendré los hooks limitados a terminales de FoxVim y preservaré las configuraciones existentes de cada agente.

    ---
    name: kl-javascript_typescript
    description: "Apply KL's JavaScript and TypeScript preferences when writing, reviewing, or refactoring scripts and applications: explicit types, semantic casing, practical classes, and concise implementation."
    metadata:
      display-name: KL-javascript_typescript
      version: "1.0.1"
    ---
    
    # JavaScript + TypeScript
    
    JavaScript/TypeScript implementation, naming, tooling; project conventions.
    
    Conditions/exceptions apply; preferences remain preferences.
    
    # ALWAYS
    
    - New projects: TypeScript almost always; Bun package manager. Existing JS/simple scripts stay JS when migration adds unrelated work; existing package manager/tooling/runtime/module conventions retained. JavaScript → explicit typed JSDoc always.
    - Prefer explicit parameter/public-return types and visibility; local annotations when clarifying domain meaning. Strictest checking; new TS configs prefer `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, `noImplicitOverride`, `noFallthroughCasesInSwitch`.
    - Typed objects=data; classes=behavior/injected collaborators; interfaces=replaceable IO. Meaningful unions/enums/domain types for states/identifiers. Concrete helpers; generics only for actual multitype need. `Partial`/`Pick`/`Omit`/`satisfies` for intended shapes; named options when positional arguments obscure calls.
    - Readable branches/early returns/straight transformations; cohesive stateless static helpers or standalone functions acceptable. Non-null assertions permitted when business logic guarantees non-nullness.
    - Prefer PascalCase public domain members/methods/classes/exported helpers; camelCase private/protected implementation/parameters/locals; SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE immutable global config. Preserve external shapes/framework contracts/existing APIs.
    - Interface `I` prefix; project prefix for domain entities when useful. Prefer one main class/file, feature-local domain types, colocated tests; existing filenames/exports (including main-class default exports) respected.
    - Short intent/invariant comments; JSDoc example when clarifying utility contract. New tooling: prefer Biome, tabs, double quotes, semicolons, 120 columns.
    - Prefer erased-type `import type`; decorator-DI classes requiring runtime metadata retain value imports and narrowly scoped lint exceptions.
    
    # NEVER
    
    - No `any` in TypeScript or JavaScript/JSDoc.
    - No npm/pnpm/Yarn for new projects; existing project manager is the exception.
    - No mechanical obvious-type repetition, class per data object, interface per class, speculative generics, or clever expressions/layers over readable logic.
    - No casing-only existing API renames; no `KL` prefix in unrelated projects; no competing formatter.
    - No `import type` conversion for DI classes required as runtime metadata.
